## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck keeps one visual language (black ground, gold and green accents, sparse titles) and uses large, well-chosen visuals, so the audience can follow a keynote route from scaling laws (5-8) to Blackwell inference (19-30), the roadmap (33-38, 44), enterprise (45-63) and robotics (64-70), closing on a summary (71). The stepped chart build on 22-28 and the one-chart-per-point slides like 9, 10 and 38 communicate a leap in performance clearly, and the roadmap on 44 is organised into legible rows and columns. Weaknesses are small type on several slides (the annotation labels on 27-28, the enterprise architecture diagrams on 53-63, the logo wall on 2), dim de-emphasised series on 26, and a few near-empty or low-information pages (20, 69, 72), which suggest the deck depends on a speaker and video for full effect.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the dark, restrained visual system, the single-message slide structure, the stepped chart reveal on 22-28 and the clean generation comparison on 44.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or cut the tiny annotation text on 27-28 and the partner diagrams on 53-63, and brighten the dimmed series and labels on 25-26.

**What would change the score:** I would add a short agenda or section marker and replace the placeholder-like pages (20, 69, 72) with stills that stand alone.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 73 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is the keynote presentation from NVIDIA GTC 2025. It opens with headline growth figures for Hopper and Blackwell GPUs and data center revenue, then frames the industry around a computing inflection point. The middle sections cover the Blackwell system and inference performance, shown through a series of incremental benchmark charts that build from Hopper to Blackwell NVL72 with Dynamo. Further sections present the product roadmap through Blackwell Ultra, Vera Rubin and Rubin Ultra, followed by networking and co-packaged photonics, enterprise AI infrastructure and workstations, Llama Nemotron models, Omniverse and Cosmos for physical AI, and Isaac GR00T robotics. It closes with a summary slide restating the main announcements. Many slides use a single large figure or a stepped chart to carry the argument, and several roadmap slides compare generations side by side.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides are in the NVIDIA GTC 2025 keynote deck?**

The deck has 73 slides, and slide 1 is the NVIDIA title slide. The slides run in order from slide 1 through slide 73.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about the Blackwell 72 system specifications?**

Slide 18, titled NVIDIA Blackwell System 72, lists 72 Blackwell GPUs, 1.1 EF FP4, 20 TB of memory, and 130 Trillion Transistors. It also lists 18 NVLink Switches and 72 ConnectX-8 NICs.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about the Rubin Ultra NVL576 roadmap?**

Slide 35 gives Rubin Ultra NVL576 with a timing of Second Half 2027, 15 EF FP4 Inference, and 5 EF FP8 Training. It also lists 4 Reticle-Sized GPUs and 1TB HBM4e.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about Blackwell and Hopper GPU shipments to top US cloud providers?**

Slide 9, titled Top 4 US CSPs, shows 3.6M Blackwell GPUs So Far (2-GPUs per Chip) and 1.3M Hopper GPUs. The chart compares 2024 and 2025.

### Slide 3

**Text**

GEFORCE RTX 5090

**Layout**

A single full-bleed photograph fills the slide with no title, body text, footer or slide number. The product photo sits inset in a rounded frame with a thin gold outline, leaving a narrow black margin on all sides, with the cards occupying the lower-right and upper-left of the frame.

**Visuals**

One photograph of two NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5090 graphics cards, rendered as a product shot. At back left is a light grey, beige-white shroud (the card's cooler cover) with the embossed text 'GEFORCE RTX 5090' running diagonally across its top surface, grille vents along its side ends and chevron-shaped indents on its top. In the foreground right is a black Founders-Edition-style card with two five-blade fans set in rounded square silver-chrome frames joined by an X-shaped chrome cross. Lighting is soft studio light with specular highlights on the chrome rims and fan hubs. Dominant colours are black, light grey and silver. Composition is a low three-quarter angle with shallow depth of field, the background blurred. The photo is not cropped inside its frame; it is boxed with rounded corners and a thin gold outline, and it sits on a black surround.

**Design**

Background: Solid near-black #0A0A0A, covered by a full-bleed dark photograph. Black background (#0A0A0A) with no text or graphic design elements. The only colour accent is the thin gold/tan outline (about #C8B98A) around the framed photo, with small rounded corners (about 1% of slide width). No typography is set on the slide apart from the embossed product lettering. **Text**

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with no visible margins or footer and no slide number. The title 'FROM ONE TO THREE SCALING LAWS' sits centred in the upper-left-centre area (about x 28 to 45, y 19 to 26). A vertical y-axis runs at x 22 from y 22 to 86, and a horizontal x-axis runs along y 85 from x 22 to x 75, with four stage labels sitting on it. The right-hand verifier loop occupies x 59 to 88, y 34 to 70. Elements are free-placed, not on a grid.

**Visuals**

Hand-drawn chalk-style diagram in white and green on black. (1) Y-axis: a single white chalk line, vertical, from about y 22 to y 86 at x 22, labelled '“INTELLIGENCE”' (no units, no numeric scale, no tick marks). (2) X-axis: a white chalk baseline from the y-axis corner at (22, 85) running right to about x 75, with four stage labels in capitals (PERCEPTION AI, GENERATIVE AI, AGENTIC AI, PHYSICAL AI) and white arrowheads on the connectors between them (from PERCEPTION to GENERATIVE, GENERATIVE to AGENTIC, AGENTIC to PHYSICAL); the line after PHYSICAL AI ends at about x 80 with no arrowhead. (3) Three green chalk arrows rising to the upper right, all starting near (27, 86): the longest reaches about (65, 12) and is labelled TEST-TIME SCALING / 'LONG THINKING'; a middle arrow ends about (56, 40) and a shorter arrow ends about (46, 59); the labels are POST-TRAINING SCALING (on the middle branch) and PRE-TRAINING SCALING (near the origin). No numeric values, no legend, no highlighted data points. (4) Process diagram on the right: a rounded rectangle 'VERIFIER' at top, a rounded rectangle 'MODEL' below it, and a rounded rectangle 'PROBLEM PROMPTS' to the left of MODEL. A white arrow points down from VERIFIER to MODEL, a white arrow points right from PROBLEM PROMPTS to MODEL, and a white loop arrow leaves the right side of MODEL, curves up along x 81 and returns with an arrowhead into the right side of VERIFIER, labelled ANSWER beside the loop. The caption POST-TRAINING WITH RLVR / >100T TOKENS sits below the boxes. Boxes have white chalk outlines about 2 px wide with rounded corners and black interiors. Measured images: a full-slide 4000 by 2250 pixel image forming the black background, and about 23 small image-based strokes and label pieces (arrows, box outlines, the axis lines and the stage labels) placed as listed in the elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide black image; a white #FFFFFF fill is recorded beneath it but is not visible). Black chalkboard background (#000000) with white chalk strokes (#FFFFFF) for axes, arrows, boxes, and most text. Green accent #76B900 for the three scaling-law arrows and their labels. Typeface: a hand-drawn, slightly irregular rounded sans-serif resembling a chalk handwriting font, regular weight, uppercase throughout. Title cap height about 2.7 percent of slide height (em about 3.8 percent). Body and labels cap height about 1.8 percent (em about 2.5 percent). **Layout**

A single centred chart group on a black field. The title 'Top 4 US CSPs' sits centred above the chart at about 20% from the top. The two-bar chart sits in the centre with its baseline at about 81% of the slide height and the year labels beneath it. The 2024 callout is left of its bar and the 2025 callout is right of its bar, each joined by a dashed leader line. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart with two bars, titled 'Top 4 US CSPs'. There is no y-axis, no gridlines and no axis title. The horizontal x-axis is a thin dark grey line running from about x 42.1% to 58.3% at y 80.9%. Category labels below the axis: 2024 and 2025. Series: one series of GPU shipments with two labelled values. 2024 bar labelled 1.3M, callout 'Hopper GPUs'. 2025 bar labelled 3.6M, callout 'Blackwell GPUs So Far (2-GPUs per Chip)'. Bar heights are approximately proportional to the values: the 2024 bar is about 17% of slide height tall and the 2025 bar about 48% tall. Both bars are filled with a vertical gradient from pale cream at the top to dark olive-brown at the bottom. The 2025 bar is the tallest and brightest. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the tallest bar.

**Design**

Background is solid black. Title and large figures use a muted gold colour #C1B485. Callout descriptions and year labels are white #FFFFFF. Typeface is NVIDIASans, a sans-serif, Medium weight for the title and figures and Regular weight for labels. Title and figures are about 3.3% of slide height; labels and years are about 2.2% of slide height. Leader lines are thin dashed grey #6E6E6E, about 0.1% high. Bars have a sharp rectangular outline with no corner radius, no shadow, and a vertical gradient from #F3EDDC at top through #B3A77F to #5A4E36 at bottom.

**Layout**

A single centred chart composition on a black slide. The title is centred horizontally near the top at about 13% from the top edge. The bar chart fills the middle band, with the y-axis labels in a column at the left, the category years on a baseline near 81% of the height, and a vertical legend at the right edge of the chart, starting at about 78% of the width. No footer, source line beyond the legend note, or slide number is visible. Text is left-aligned in the legend and y-axis labels and centred on the bars for the years.

**Visuals**

Chart: grouped and single bar chart with two series, titled 'Computing At Inflection Point'. Y-axis: linear, labelled $1,000, $750, $500 and $250 with gridlines absent, range $0 to $1,000 (baseline at $0 on the horizontal axis line). X-axis: years 2022 to 2028, one category per year. Series 1 'NVDA DC Revenue' (beige bars with gradient, only for 2022 to 2024): 2022 about $15, 2023 about $45, 2024 about $100; no printed values. Series 2 'Data Center Capex (Dell’Oro Projections)' (purple gradient bars, all years): 2022 about $235, 2023 about $260, 2024 about $395, 2025 about $515, 2026 about $655, 2027 about $775, 2028 about $880; the 2028 value is annotated only by the text '$1T+' above it, and no values are printed on the bars. Legend: at right, beige square for NVDA DC Revenue, purple square for Data Center Capex with the note (Dell’Oro Projections) beneath. Highlighted points: the years 2022, 2023 and 2024 are colour-highlighted in beige and enclosed by a thin beige outlined rounded rectangle, while 2025 to 2028 are white. A thin dark purple arrow runs in two segments from just above the 2022 purple bar up and to the right, with arrowheads, ending above the 2028 bar near the '$1T+' label. Several image elements are placed over the chart area; they carry the bar and arrow graphics.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background. Plain black background, no gradients on the slide itself. Title in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans Medium, regular case, about 3.7% of slide height, centred. Axis labels and legend text in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.2% of slide height for axis and year labels and about 2.0% for legend entries, about 1.5% for the Dell’Oro note. Accent colour for the '$1T+' label and 2022 to 2024 year labels is warm beige #C1B485. NVDA bars use a beige gradient from light #E0D5AE at top to #A39468 at base. Data center capex bars use a vertical purple gradient from #6A3D9A at top to #2E0B4F at base, with thin darker purple edges. Bars have small square tops and no corner radius. Baseline is a hairline grey-white rule about 0.1% of slide height. Year highlight box is a 0.1% stroke beige #C1B485 rounded rectangle with a small radius, no fill and no shadow. Arrow is dark purple #2A0B45 with a thin stroke.

**Text**

CUDA-X FOR EVERY INDUSTRY / WARP / PHYSICS / cuDF / cuML / DATA SCIENCE AND PROCESSING / cuDSS / cuSPARSE / cuFFT / AMGX / COMPUTER AIDED ENGINEERING / TRT-LLM / MEGATRON / NCCL / cuDNN / CUTLASS / cuBLAS / DEEP LEARNING / cuEQUIVARIANCE / cuTENSOR / QUANTUM CHEMISTRY / cuQUANTUM / CUDA-Q / QUANTUM COMPUTING / EARTH-2 / WEATHER ANALYTICS / MONAI / MEDICAL IMAGING / PARABRICKS / GENE SEQUENCING / cuOPT / DECISION OPTIMIZATION / AERIAL / SIONNA / 5G/6G / SIGNAL PROCESSING / cuLITHO / COMPUTATIONAL LITHOGRAPHY / cuPYNUMERIC / NUMERICAL COMPUTING

**Layout**

A single centred title sits near the top at about 13.5% from the top of the slide, with a horizontal row of 13 equal-height hand-sketched cards spanning the full width below it, slightly tilted and staggered in height. Each card is followed by a centred label block set beneath it, with no footer, logo or slide number visible. Margins are roughly 1.7% on the left and right edges.

**Visuals**

Thirteen hand-sketched rounded-rectangle cards with white chalk-like outlines, each containing an illustration on a black ground, left to right: (1) WARP: swirling green, blue and magenta fluid vortices with white line sketches, tall card, about 15% wide. (2) cuDF/cuML: sparse scattered dots in purple, green and blue on black, arranged in loose clusters. (3) cuDSS/cuFFT/AMGX: grey-white smoky streaks and a jet-engine turbine drawn in white and yellow-green particles. (4) TRT-LLM/Deep learning: a dark blue gradient background with a white line drawing of a server rack and a connected component. (5) cuEQUIVARIANCE/QUANTUM CHEMISTRY: a ball-and-stick molecule with teal, blue, red and grey spheres. (6) cuQUANTUM: a glowing gold cylindrical cryostat with white line detail. (7) EARTH-2: a dark blue globe showing the Americas with green and magenta tints. (8) MONAI: a torso and organ scan in teal, green and orange. (9) PARABRICKS: dark blue-grey cloud-like clusters with purple and orange accents. (10) cuOPT: a glowing green node with a blue network of lines and points. (11) AERIAL SIONNA: white outlined buildings with bright green radio ray lines and a yellow lamp shape. (12) cuLITHO: a white outlined chip-layout pattern with gold details. (13) cuPYNUMERIC: a purple, blue and white spiral galaxy. Below each card, a label block: a gold (#E6C27A) product name line group and white (#F2F2F2) sub-label, centred. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide black image covers the white fill behind it). Pure black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) hand-drawn chalk-style outlines. Title in white (#FFFFFF) hand-drawn sans-serif, all caps, regular weight, about 4% of slide height. Product names in gold (#E6C27A), sub-labels in white (#F2F2F2), handwritten-style sans-serif all caps, about 2% of slide height. Card outlines are white sketchy strokes about 0.3% of slide height thick, with rounded corners and no shadows. Cards are slightly rotated and vary in height.

### Slide 12

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Full-bleed single image with no text; the globe is centred horizontally and vertically on a black field, with the disc occupying roughly the middle 50% of the slide width and nearly the full height. No title, footer, or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style rendering of planet Earth as a full sphere viewed from above the North Pole region, showing North America, Greenland, the Arctic, the Caribbean, Central America and northern South America. Subject: the whole Earth disc with a thin bright blue atmospheric rim glowing at the edge. Snow and ice cover northern Canada and the Arctic archipelago, Hudson Bay appears as a dark patch, and green aurora-like glow is visible over northeastern Canada and the North Atlantic. The western United States and Mexico are brown and tan, the Gulf of Mexico and Caribbean are deep navy, and the Atlantic and Pacific are dark blue with white cloud streaks. Composition: the sphere is centred, slightly tilted, with the terminator not visible; lighting is even daylight from the upper left, giving a soft highlight on the disc. Dominant colours: black background, navy #0B2A4A to deep blue #1E4D8C ocean, tan #8A6A4A land, white #F2F2F2 ice. The image is not cropped; the globe is fully within the frame. No overlay or tint; the image covers the slide edge to edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with the full-slide Earth photograph placed over it.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100: Full-slide rendering of Earth as a sphere centred on the slide, showing North America and the Arctic, with the atmospheric rim visible (Image is 4000 by 2250 pixels and also a 2200 by 1238 pixel version, both covering the slide; the globe disc sits roughly from x 24 to 76 and y 4 to 96 of the slide. No crop, no border, no rounded corners, no shadow; the surrounding field is black #000000. Glow at the rim is pale blue #5FA8E8.)

A rounded outline panel with a thin tan #C1B485 stroke and a black fill sits to the right of the pie, containing four centred items: Leading Technology, Full-Stack AI, CUDA-X Diverse Applications, Rich Developer Ecosystem. ### Slide 14

**Text**

gm / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Two logos sit side by side in a top band, centred either side of a thin vertical hairline at the slide's horizontal centre, with the GM mark left of centre and the NVIDIA wordmark right of centre. A dark sketch of two vehicles fills the lower three quarters of the slide, spanning nearly the full width with margins of about 7.5% on the left and 6% on the right. There is no title, footer, slide number or other text.

**Visuals**

Three non-text visuals. (1) A full-bleed near-black background image (4000 by 2250 px) covering the whole slide. (2) A monochrome white-line sketch illustration, placed at 7.5%, 24.9%, 86.7% by 75.1%, on a black field so its edges blend into the background, with no box or border. The left vehicle is a large SUV in three-quarter front view facing right, with a rectangular grille, a crest emblem on the grille, slim vertical LED headlamps, and wheels at the bottom. The right vehicle is a smaller electric SUV in three-quarter front view facing left, with a chevron-patterned grille, a crest emblem, and thin vertical lighting strips at each side. Both are drawn in hatched, sketchy white and grey strokes with warm gold-amber rim highlights along their roof and shoulder lines. Lighting is dramatic and low-key, from the upper edges, with the rest in shadow. Dominant colours are black, white and warm gold. (3) Two logos: the General Motors 'gm' mark, a rounded-square outline containing lowercase 'gm' in a bright sky-blue; and the NVIDIA logo, a green eye-shaped emblem followed by the white bold geometric wordmark 'NVIDIA'. A thin vertical grey hairline separates the two logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide image. Pure black background with no gradient, pattern or texture. The only text is the logo wordmarks; the 'gm' mark is sky blue (#1EA1F5) and the NVIDIA eye is green (#76B900) with a white (#FFFFFF) wordmark. The vehicle sketch uses white (#FFFFFF) and grey (#8A8A8A) linework with warm gold (#C8A060) highlights. The divider is a hairline in dark grey (#555555), about 1 px wide. No shadows, corner radii beyond the logo's own rounded square, or overlays.

NVIDIA Blackwell System

**Layout**

Single centred composition on a full black field. The title sits centred across the top at about 6.7% from the top edge. A large circular grey-white backdrop is centred on the slide, with a square chip graphic centred inside it and a caption banner below the chip. Four stat callouts sit on the left and four on the right, each attached to a gold dot on a thin gold arc that wraps around the chip. A server-rack image sits at the bottom left, overlapping the left arc. No footer, no slide number, no logos.

**Visuals**

1) Circular backdrop photograph, about 43% of slide width, centred at x 28.5 to 71.5 and y 14.5 to 90.9. It shows a blurred, marbled, brushed-metal abstract in silver, white and charcoal, with bright diagonal streaks running from upper right to lower left. Circular crop, full opacity, no overlay. 2) Chip graphic, a top-down render of the Grace Blackwell NVLink72 die, placed as a square boxed image at x 38.7 to 61.3 horizontally, spanning about 24.2% to 75.8% in the wider square frame. It shows a dense grid of small silver and grey square tiles, with rows of large compute blocks, a central horizontal band of smaller tiles and interconnect traces, memory-tile rows above and below, and a black border around the die. Lighting is soft and even, dominant colours are silver, grey and black. The whole die is visible, not cropped. 3) Thin gold circular arcs, about 0.1% stroke, #C1B485, forming two partial rings: a left arc from about x 29 y 22 down to x 29 y 81 and a right arc from about x 68 y 22 down to x 67 y 81, both outside the circle. 4) Eight small gold spheres as markers on the arcs, each about 1% of slide width, with a metallic gold highlight: left at about x 24.8 y 43.1, x 24.1 y 52.8 and x 24.8 y 63.2; right at about x 75.2 y 43.1, x 75.7 y 49.8, x 75.7 y 56.4 and x 75.2 y 63.2. 5) Server rack image, a tall dark enclosure with horizontal gold-lit trays and black chassis, boxed, unrounded, placed at x 28.7 to 34.3 and y 71.5 to 100 (bottom edge cropped by slide bottom), overlapping the left arc. 6) Caption banner image behind the text 'Grace Blackwell NVLink72', a dark grey gradient bar fading to transparent at its ends, at x 38.7 to 60.7 and y 77.3 to 86.3.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with no gradient or pattern behind the elements. Pure black background with no gradient. Title in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans-Medium, a clean grotesque sans-serif, about 3.33% of slide height, regular case. Stat numbers in gold #C1B485, NVIDIASans-Medium, about 1.98% of slide height. Stat labels in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans-Regular, about 1.98% of slide height. Caption in white, sans-serif, about 1.98% of slide height. Accent colour is gold #C1B485 for numbers, arcs and dots. Shapes have no rounded corners or shadows; arcs are hairline-thin. Left labels are right-aligned, right labels are left-aligned.

**Layout**

Single centred title sits at the top of the slide, above a large line chart whose axes form an L in the lower-left of the plot area. Axis labels sit beside and below the axes, with the horizontal x-axis arrow ending in the 'Smart AI Fast Response' label near the right end of the axis; no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Conceptual line chart with no printed numeric scale, so no axis values are shown. Vertical axis labelled 'Throughput' with an upward white arrow; horizontal axis labelled 'Smart AI Fast Response' with a rightward white arrow, and a second caption 'Tokens per Second For One User' under the axis. A gold curve descends from upper left (about 28% x, 23% y) to lower right (about 77% x, 74% y) and is the highlighted curve; its middle section carries a bright white-gold glow. Above it, a gold arrow pointing up-right is labelled 'Revenue'. A dimmer grey curve starts about 28% x, 50% y and descends to about 39% x, 74% y; it is labelled 'Tokens per Second For Factory' on the vertical side, with a gold arrow pointing up-right at the FLOPS label, which lists the drivers 'FLOPS, HBM Memory, HBM Bandwidth, Software and Architecture'. Behind the curves is a dim photograph of rows of server racks in perspective.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a dim, full-bleed photograph of server racks shown behind the chart. Dark, cinematic keynote-style slide. Background pure black #000000. Title in white #FFFFFF NVIDIASans-Medium, sans-serif, regular case, about 3.3% of slide height. Labels in white #FFFFFF NVIDIASans-Regular, sans-serif, about 2.0% of slide height (FLOPS list about 1.7%). Accent gold #B8A46A for the highlighted curve and the two gold arrows; grey #9B9B9B for the secondary curve; axes in dark grey #5A5A5A hairlines, about 0.1% of slide width thick. Gold curve has a soft glow of #FFF3C4 near its middle. No shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 20

**Text**

I need to seat 7 people around a table at my wedding reception, but my parents and in-laws should not sit next to each other. Also, my wife insists we look better in pictures when she's on my left, but I need to sit next to my best man. How do I seat us on a round table? But then, what happens if we invited our pastor to sit with us?
Send
Traditional LLM Model
Tokens: 439
Reasoning Model
Tokens: 8,559
Reasoning On
20x More Tokens
150x More Compute

**Layout**

A single centred prompt box spans the top of the slide, with two equal-width columns below it, a red-bordered empty panel on the left and a green-bordered panel on the right, each with a bold heading and token count above. Titles are centred over their panels, there is no footer, page number or logo, and the margins are about 3% on the sides.

**Visuals**

Left: an empty rectangle with a red outline (about #D00000, roughly 2px stroke, small corner radius) and black fill, containing no content. Right: a rectangle with a green outline (#76B900, roughly 2px stroke, small corner radius) and black fill, containing a green toggle switch (filled #76B900 pill with a black circular knob on the right) labelled 'Reasoning On' and the centred bold text '20x More Tokens / 150x More Compute'. Top box: a paper-plane icon in black on the green Send pill button. No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background with NVIDIA-style green #76B900 accents and a red #D00000 frame on the left panel. Titles and the big statement are white (#FFFFFF) or light grey (#E6E6E6) bold sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans-like) at about 3 to 5% of slide height; prompt text is an italic sans-serif in off-white (#F0F0F0) at about 2% of slide height. Token labels are grey (#A0A0A0) with numbers in green. Shapes have thin outlines with small rounded corners and no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Announcing NVIDIA Dynamo / Distributed Inference Serving Library / Query / Prefill / KV $ / Decode / Disaggregated Inference / GPU Resource Allocation / KV Cache Routing / Communication Library (NIXL) / cohere / Fireworks AI / Meta / Microsoft Azure / perplexity / PyTorch / SGL / together.ai / VAST / vLLM

**Layout**

Centred composition on a black field. The title block sits at the top centre (title line above a subtitle line). A stack of five rounded cards sits in the middle, with the front card holding a Query-to-Prefill-to-KV-cache-to-Decode flow and a row of four outlined label chips below it. A row of ten partner logos runs across the bottom, spread evenly from about 10.7% to 88.6% of slide width. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Illustration of a distributed inference pipeline on a black background. Four dark-grey rounded cards are stacked behind a larger front card, offset up and to the left to suggest depth. On the front card, a 'Query' label with a thin grey arrow points right into a gold-bronze chip icon labelled 'Prefill' (a square processor with a grid of small gold cells, framed by a gold bezel). A second thin arrow runs to a rectangular gold-bronze block labelled 'KV $' with a bevelled, metallic look. A third arrow runs to a second gold chip icon labelled 'Decode', identical in style to the Prefill chip. From the Decode chip, a thin grey arrow path rises, bends right and drops down onto a row of five small squares: the first is solid white and the other four are mid-grey (#5A5A5A-ish). A grey loop path leaves the bottom of the square row, bends left and returns upward with an arrowhead into the bottom of the Decode chip, forming a feedback loop. A final thin grey arrow leaves the right end of the square row and points right to the card edge. The front card has a thin gold-grey stroke. Four outlined label chips below the card read: Disaggregated Inference, GPU Resource Allocation, KV Cache Routing, Communication Library (NIXL). Lighting is flat with a soft metallic highlight on the gold chips. Composition is centred and horizontally symmetrical. Nothing is cropped or full-bleed except the black background.

**Design**

Background is solid black, with a subtle dark tone on the cards. Title text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Medium, a neo-grotesque sans-serif, at about 3.3% of slide height. Subtitle is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 2.2% of slide height. Diagram labels 'Query', 'Prefill', 'Decode', 'KV $' are white #FFFFFF, about 1.5 to 1.7% of slide height, with Prefill and Decode in bold. Chip labels are gold-beige #B2A480 in NVIDIA Sans Medium, about 1.7% of slide height. Chip outlines are thin gold-beige #B2A480 strokes about 1px with fill #0D0D0D and corner radius of about 3px. Card strokes are thin grey #3A3A3A on the back cards and a gold-grey #8C8270 on the front card. Front card has rounded corners of about 2% of slide width. Arrows are thin grey #9A9A9A lines with small triangular heads. Logos are white monochrome.

**Layout**

A single chart slide on a full-bleed black field. The title is centred horizontally near the top (about 9% from the top), with the chart's y-axis running up the left-centre and its x-axis along the lower part of the plot, with axis titles beneath it. Arrow-and-label pairs sit outside each axis at the left and bottom-right; there is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Chart: a line-with-markers chart, no title of its own. Y-axis labelled Throughput (arrow pointing up) and TPS / MW, range 0 to 10,000,000 with ticks every 1,000,000 (labels 0, 1,000,000 to 10,000,000). X-axis labelled TPS for 1 User and Smart AI Fast Response (arrow pointing right), range 0 to about 650 with ticks at 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600. One series, gold #C1B485 line with round gold markers, values estimated (no values printed): about (5, 2,360,000), (17, 2,020,000), (28, 1,660,000), (40, 1,180,000), (43, 790,000), (51, 750,000), (74, 530,000), (80, 280,000), (102, 80,000), (118, 20,000), (170, about 0), (253, about 0). Series is labelled Hopper in gold text beside the point near (28, 1,660,000) with the label placed right and above. No legend. No points highlighted beyond the Hopper label. Two small gold arrow images: an upward arrow beside the Throughput label and a rightward arrow beside Smart AI Fast Response; both are solid gradient gold shafts with triangular heads.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed image covering the entire slide. Solid black background, no gradient or texture. Title in white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Medium, sans-serif, roughly 3.3% of slide height, normal weight, sentence-style capitalisation. Axis tick labels and axis titles in white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Regular, sans-serif, about 1.5% of slide height for ticks and 2.0% for axis titles. Accent colour gold #C1B485 used for the data line, markers, Hopper label and both arrows. Axis lines are thin grey hairlines approximately #8C8C8C, about 1 px wide, no shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 25

**Text**

Blackwell Giant Leap in Inference Performance / Throughput / 10,000,000 / 9,000,000 / 8,000,000 / 7,000,000 / 6,000,000 / TPS / MW / 5,000,000 / 4,000,000 / 3,000,000 / Blackwell / NVL8 / FP8 / Blackwell / NVL8 / FP4 / Blackwell / NVL72 / FP4 / Hopper / 2,000,000 / 1,000,000 / 0 / 0 / 100 / 200 / 300 / 400 / 500 / 600 / Smart AI / TPS for 1 User / Fast Response

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a full-bleed black field. Title is centred horizontally in the top band at about 9% from the top. The chart plot sits in the middle with a left y-axis and a bottom x-axis, with rotated axis titles at the left, and the x-axis title and two-line 'Smart AI / Fast Response' label below the baseline. Both axes carry direction arrows, and the slide has no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Line chart with markers. Title: Blackwell Giant Leap in Inference Performance. Y axis: Throughput, labelled TPS / MW, 0 to 10,000,000 in steps of 1,000,000 (ticks at 0, 1,000,000, 2,000,000, 3,000,000, 4,000,000, 5,000,000, 6,000,000, 7,000,000, 8,000,000, 9,000,000, 10,000,000). X axis: TPS for 1 User, 0 to 600 in steps of 100 (ticks 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600), with a second axis label Smart AI / Fast Response on the arrow. No values are printed on the points, so all values below are estimates marked about. Series 1, Blackwell NVL72 FP4, gold line #C1B485 with gold markers, the brightest series: about (12, 9.5M), (25, 9.3M), (47, 9.05M), (80, 8.45M), (100, 8.1M), (136, 7.45M), (213, 5.8M), (270, 3.7M), (310, 0.25M), (326, 0.15M), (372, 0.08M), (407, 0.03M). Series 2, Blackwell NVL8 FP4, dark olive line #514929 with dark markers: about (15, 8.7M), (20, 8.7M), (27, 8.6M), (37, 8.4M), (40, 7.95M), (45, 7.7M), (65, 7.45M), (80, 6.8M), (100, 5.8M), (120, 3.75M), (165, 0.6M), (190, 0.3M), (250, 0.05M), (290, 0.02M), (310, 0.01M). Series 3, Blackwell NVL8 FP8, dark olive line with dark markers: about (5, 4.95M), (25, 4.9M), (30, 4.8M), (42, 4.8M), (50, 4.25M), (65, 3.8M), (85, 2.45M), (100, 1.45M), (115, 0.8M), (120, 0.4M), (130, 0.1M), (130, 0.2M), (110, 0.25M), (100, 0.05M), (120, 0.02M). Series 4, Hopper, dark olive line with dark markers: about (5, 2.35M), (15, 2.05M), (30, 1.7M), (40, 1.2M), (42, 0.8M), (50, 0.75M), (70, 0.5M), (80, 0.3M), (100, 0.05M), (120, 0.02M). Legend: none; series are identified by direct labels Blackwell NVL72 FP4 (gold, right of the gold line near 270 to 330 on x and 3M to 2M on y), Blackwell NVL8 FP4 and Blackwell NVL8 FP8 (dark, centred near x 130 and x 85 at about 3M), and Hopper (dark, about x 80 at 1.5M). Highlighted points: the NVL72 FP4 series is the only highlighted series, drawn in gold with the lighter markers. Gridlines: none. Up arrow: a gold tan vertical arrow with a triangular head at the top of the y-axis title, pointing up. Right arrow: a gold tan horizontal arrow with a triangular head at the right end of the x-axis label, pointing right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background. Pure black background with white sans-serif text. Title is white NVIDIASans Medium, set in regular sentence-style capitalisation with no capitals styling. Axis tick labels and axis titles are white NVIDIASans Regular. Gold accent #C1B485 for the highest series, its label, and the NVL72 FP4 text; dark olive #514929 for the other series and labels. Lines are about 0.3 percent of slide height thick with round markers about 1 percent of slide width. Axis lines are thin grey #444444. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

**Layout**

Single chart on a full-bleed black field with the title centred across the top at about 9 percent of slide height. The plot sits to the right of a rotated y-axis title and above a centred x-axis title, with the x-axis title and the arrow to its right at the bottom. A vertical up arrow sits at the left beside the Throughput label and a horizontal right arrow sits at the lower right beside Smart AI / Fast Response. Left margin about 14 percent, right margin about 16 percent, no footer, no slide number.

**Visuals**

Line chart of connected point series with no internal title and no legend; series are labelled directly on the plot. Y axis: TPS / MW (also captioned Throughput by the up arrow), linear, 0 to 10,000,000, gridlines absent, ticks every 1,000,000 with labels at 0, 1,000,000 through 10,000,000. X axis: TPS for 1 User (with Smart AI / Fast Response arrow), linear, 0 to about 640, ticks labelled 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600. (6) A dark series from about 5.0 million at about 5 TPS through about 4.8 million at about 30, about 4.3 million at about 45, about 3.8 million at about 65, falling to about 0 near 250 TPS. Dark series end markers cluster near 370 to 410 TPS and near 0 in 250 to 290 TPS. Markers are filled circles about 1 percent of slide width in size. Background: Solid black #000000, full-bleed image covering the whole slide. Pure black background (#000000) with no gradient or texture. **Layout**

Centered header block at top: title and two subtitle lines stacked and centred on the slide horizontal axis. Below it a single chart fills the middle and lower area: vertical y-axis at about x 23.6% with tick labels right-aligned to its left, horizontal x-axis at about y 78.7% with tick labels centred beneath, and axis titles on both axes (rotated 'Throughput' and 'TPS / MW' on the left, 'TPS for 1 User' and 'Smart AI / Fast Response' beneath). Series labels sit to the right of the curve ends in staggered rows with leader lines. No footer, logo or slide number is visible; margins are roughly 5% left and right.

**Visuals**

One line chart (scatter-with-connecting-lines, a Pareto frontier style). Title: none inside the chart; the heading above reads Blackwell 25X Hopper. Y axis: 'Throughput', labelled 'TPS / MW', unit tokens per second per megawatt, range 0 to 10,000,000 with ticks every 1,000,000 (0, 1,000,000, 2,000,000 ... 10,000,000). X axis: 'TPS for 1 User' with ticks 0 to 600 every 100, with the 'Smart AI / Fast Response' arrow beneath pointing right, unit tokens per second per user. Series 1 (Blackwell FP4 NVL72 Dynamo, pale gold #C1B485 line and markers, upper curve): points labelled EP8 Batch 3072 Disagg Off at about x 14, y 9,500,000; EP32 Batch 1792 about x 25, y 9,300,000; EP64 Batch 896 about x 47, y 9,050,000; EP64 Batch 512 about x 79, y 8,450,000; EP64 Batch 384 about x 101, y 8,080,000; EP64 Batch 256 about x 137, y 7,430,000; EP64 Batch 128 about x 212, y 5,750,000 (highlighted with a soft glow streak); EP64+EP4 Batch 64 26% Context MTP On about x 255, y 5,080,000; EP64+EP4 Batch 32 19% Context about x 345, y 3,650,000; Batch 16 12% Context about x 393, y 2,330,000; Batch 8 7% Context about x 414, y 1,310,000; Batch 4 4% Context about x 465, y 700,000 (approximate); Batch 2 2% Context about x 485, y 400,000; TEP8EP8+EP4 Batch 4 1% Context about x 508, y 70,000; TEP8+EP4 Batch 2 1% Context about x 534, y 50,000; TEP16+EP4 Batch 2 1% Context about x 635, y 10,000 (all approximate; no values printed). Series 2 (Hopper FP8 NVL8 Dynamo, pale gold line and markers, lower curve): points about x 5, y 2,380,000; x 12, y 2,050,000; x 20, y 1,690,000; x 30, y 1,190,000; x 35, y 790,000; x 45, y 750,000; x 60, y 700,000; x 80, y 540,000; x 99, y 620,000 and further markers down to about x 240, y 0 (all approximate). Highlighted points: the glow streaks at about (Blackwell curve near x 212, y 5,750,000) and (Hopper near x 40, y 1,000,000). Legend: none. Dashed grey reference lines (#8A8A8A, 1 px) run from the y 5,750,000 point horizontally to the y axis and from the x 212 point vertically to the x axis. A dark translucent rectangle (gold tint) covers the lower-left quadrant to the reference lines, and a small dark grey dashed rectangle marks the Hopper area near the origin. Leader lines are thin grey (#5A5A5A) diagonal connectors from each point to its label.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a translucent gold tint (#C1B485, 10% opacity) over the lower-left quadrant. Black background (#000000) with white and pale gold text. Title 'Blackwell 25X Hopper' in NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, about 3.3% of slide height, centred. Subtitle lines in NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 2.2% of slide height. Axis labels and tick labels in NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 1.5% of slide height; axis titles about 2.0%. Architecture labels in NVIDIASans Medium, pale gold #C1B485, about 2.0%. Series label colours: EP8 orange #FF9900, EP32 yellow #F5E467, EP64 lime #D0FF7D with green #76B900, cyan #09DCFF and teal #009AB4 for EP64+EP4, blue #82CAFF to #0071C5 for TEP8EP8+EP4, violet #8720BA for TEP8+EP4, magenta #E91598 for TEP16+EP4. Accent: gold #C1B485 for curves and markers. Line style: 2 px gold strokes with round dot markers about 1% of slide width. Dashed reference lines are grey. No corner radii, shadows or gradients except the soft glow around highlight streaks.

### Slide 28

**Text**

Blackwell 40X Hopper / FP4, NVL72, Dynamo, and TRT-LLM Continuous Optimization / 32K ISL / 8K OSL / 1,000,000 / EP64PP2+PP4, Batch 480, 52% Context / EP64PP2+PP4, Batch 224, 50% Context / 900,000 / EP64PP2+PP4, Batch 160, 48% Context / Throughput / EP64PP4+PP4, Batch 224, 46% Context / 800,000 / EP64PP4+PP4, Batch 160, 44% Context / EP64+EP4, Batch 32, 47% Context, MTP On / 700,000 / EP64+EP4, Batch 16, 43% Context, MTP On / TPS / MW / Blackwell / EP64+EP4, Batch 8, 36% Context, MTP On / 600,000 / FP4 / Hopper / EP64+EP4, Batch 4, 29% Context, MTP On / NVL72 / FP8 / Dynamo / 500,000 / NVL8 / EP64+EP4, Batch 2, 19% Context, MTP On / Dynamo / EP64+EP4, Batch 1, 12% Context, MTP On / 400,000 / TEP4+EP4, Batch 1, 3% Context, MTP On / TEP8+EP4, Batch 1, 2%Context, MTP On / 300,000 / Smart AI / TEP16+EP4, Batch 1, 1% Context, MTP On / TPS for 1 User / Fast Response / TEP32+EP4, Batch 1, 1% Context, MTP On / 200,000 / 100,000 / 0 / 0 / 100 / 200 / 300 / 400 / 500 / 600

**Layout**

Single full-bleed chart slide with the title block centred at the top (title at about y 9 to 13 percent, subtitle lines beneath it). The chart plot area runs from about x 23.6 to 80.6 percent and y 20.5 to 78.7 percent, with the Y-axis title rotated at the far left and the X-axis titles centred below the plot. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled Blackwell 40X Hopper with subtitle FP4, NVL72, Dynamo, and TRT-LLM Continuous Optimization and 32K ISL / 8K OSL. Y axis labelled TPS / MW with a rising arrow labelled Throughput, ticks 0 to 1,000,000 in steps of 100,000. X axis labelled TPS for 1 User (ticks 0 to 600 in steps of 100) with a right-pointing arrow labelled Smart AI / Fast Response. One tan line connects about 35 markers from upper left to lower right, descending from about 860,000 TPS/MW at about 18 TPS for 1 User to near 0 at about 630 TPS for 1 User. Labelled points (values about, as no numbers are printed): EP64PP2+PP4 Batch 480 52% Context at about 18 and 860,000; Batch 224 50% Context about 30 and 830,000; Batch 160 48% Context about 45 and 800,000; EP64PP4+PP4 Batch 224 46% Context about 60 and 765,000; Batch 160 44% Context about 75 and 720,000; EP64+EP4 Batch 32 47% Context, MTP On about 95 and 630,000; Batch 16 43% Context about 155 and 625,000; Batch 8 36% Context about 235 and 525,000 (highlighted with a glowing point and a glowing streak); Batch 4 29% Context about 330 and 420,000; Batch 2 19% Context about 395 and 275,000; Batch 1 12% Context about 440 and 165,000; TEP4+EP4 Batch 1 3% Context about 465 and 48,000; TEP8+EP4 Batch 1 2% Context about 550 and 30,000; TEP16+EP4 Batch 1 1% Context about 595 and 15,000; TEP32+EP4 Batch 1 1% Context about 630 and 5,000. A second cluster of Hopper points sits near the origin: about 8 TPS and 180,000, 8 and 160,000, 15 and 130,000, 20 and 128,000, 30 and 95,000, 55 and 60,000, 65 and 40,000, 75 and 25,000, 95 and 12,000, 130 and 5,000, 165 and 3,000, 230 and 1,000. Hopper points carry a soft glow near about 27 and 97,000. Leader lines run from each marker to its label, drawn as thin grey diagonal lines. Series label colours: EP64PP2+PP4 orange #FF9900 and brown tones; EP64PP2+PP4 batch 160 yellow #F5E467; EP64PP4+PP4 lime #D0FF7D; EP64+EP4 cyan #09DCFF; TEP4+EP4 light blue #82CAFF; TEP8+EP4 purple #8720BA; TEP16+EP4 magenta #E91598; TEP32+EP4 grey #CDCDCD. Two dashed guide lines in grey mark the Batch 8 point: a horizontal dashed line at about 525,000 from the Y axis to the point and a vertical dashed line at about 235 TPS from the X axis to the point. A dark translucent box with a dashed outline covers the lower-left region up to about 235 TPS and 525,000, with a smaller dashed box at the origin up to about 30 TPS and 100,000. Region callouts: Hopper FP8 NVL8 Dynamo in tan at lower left, and Blackwell FP4 NVL72 Dynamo in tan at centre right. No legend box.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide image (a white #FFFFFF fill sits beneath it and is fully covered). Pure black background with white and tan type. Titles are white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium, about 3.3 percent of slide height; subtitle lines are white NVIDIASans Regular about 2.2 percent. Axis titles and tick labels are white NVIDIASans Regular about 1.5 to 2.0 percent. Region callouts are tan #C1B485 in NVIDIASans Medium about 2.0 percent. Line and arrows are tan #C1B485 with a soft glow on highlighted points. Series labels use colours listed under visuals, with MTP On in darker shades of their series colour. Lines are thin (about 0.2 percent of slide height) with no corner radii or hard shadows.

### Slide 29

**Text**

100 MW AI Factory / H100 NVL8 / GPU Dies / 45K / Racks / 1,400 / Data Center Productivity / 300M / DeepSeek R1 Context and Generation, , ISL=32K, OSL=8K @ Pareto optimal TPS/User

**Layout**

A full-bleed dark photograph fills the whole slide. A centered three-row comparison block sits in the lower-middle of the slide, with two center-aligned columns: the left column (centered near x 35%) holds the row labels and the right column (centered near x 50%) holds the values, with the header row at top. A small italic source line sits at the bottom-left margin (x 1.3%, y 96.5%). No title, slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of rows of black data-centre equipment: many identical dark grey rack or container units arranged in a grid and receding in perspective from the bottom-left foreground to a faded distant horizon at the top. The nearest units are sharpest and largest in the lower-left, with a fine silver-grey highlight on their front edges and small lit indicator panels (pale yellow-green and white) on the fronts. Each unit casts a soft reflection on a glossy black floor. Lighting is low-key, dark and directional, with the far rows dissolving into black. Dominant colours are black and charcoal grey with pale grey highlights. The image is not cropped in a visible way and is not boxed; it covers the full slide, with an overlay of the black background fading the top and far edges. Data table: 2 columns, 4 rows including header. Header row: '100 MW AI Factory' | 'H100 NVL8'. Row 1: 'GPU Dies' | '45K'. Row 2: 'Racks' | '1,400'. Row 3: 'Data Center Productivity' | '300M'. No chart, legend or highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 behind a full-bleed dark data-centre photograph. Black background (#000000) with a dark photograph covering it. Text is light grey #CDCDCD for the table headers, row labels and values, set in NVIDIASans (a clean geometric-humanist sans-serif): headers in Medium weight, row labels and values in Regular weight, all upright, sentence case, no letter-spacing. Table text is about 2.5% of slide height for headers and about 2.2% for body rows, so the headers are slightly larger than the rows. Source line in NVIDIASans Italic, about 1.5% of slide height, colour #7F7F7F. No shapes, rules, rounded corners, shadows or gradients are visible apart from the photo's dark fade.

Left of the doors is a grey cabinet, and red and blue insulated pipes run down the top left. Overhead, black cable bundles drop from green and grey cable trays and wire mesh trays that run across the ceiling, with blue pipework above. Right side: further racks with gold-coloured cable connectors. The camera is at eye level, centred on the racks, with the aisle receding slightly to the left. Lighting is even and cool, with soft highlights on the metal. Dominant colours: black, charcoal grey, cream/gold, red, blue and green. The photo is not cropped, is boxed by rounded corners, and has no overlay or tint. No charts, tables, logos apart from the NVIDIA logos on the glass doors, or icons.

Three-zone layout on a black field: a tall Oberon rack photograph fills the left column (about x 7.6 to 28.6), a centred stack of title and statistics sits in the middle column (about x 37 to 62), and a right column (about x 67 to 90) holds the Grace chip at top and the Blackwell Ultra chip with its specification lines at bottom. The title sits centred in the middle column at about y 29; there is no footer or slide number visible, and the small italic caption sits bottom-left beside the rack at about y 89.

**Visuals**

1) Photograph of an Oberon liquid-cooled rack (NVIDIA NVL72 Blackwell Ultra), tall portrait, occupying about x 7.6 to 28.6 and full height y 0 to 100 on a black ground; the rack has a dark charcoal frame and dark cabinet with gold-coloured compute trays (gold #C1B485-like brass tones) stacked in rows, with black cable bundles running down the sides; soft studio lighting, frontal view, centred composition, not cropped, boxed by the black background. 2) Photograph of the Grace CPU chip, square, about x 67.8 to 97.8 region in the top right area (image box about x 67.8 y 9.7 w 30 h 30 at 1200x675 pixels, the chip itself sits centre of a black square package with a dark bezel); the die shows a multicolour (green, orange, magenta) grid-like pattern lit from the centre. 3) Photograph of the Blackwell Ultra GPU package, about x 76.4 y 59.5 w 12.1 h 18.4; a gold-metal heatsink-style lid with four HBM stacks on top and bottom edges and a central die with a multicolour (pink, green, purple) pattern; it is boxed, not cropped, lit from above. 4) Raster image strips at about x 37.7 spanning the statistic lines (y 43.6 to 77.7), duplicating the text rows; they are visually identical to the text lines.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-bleed black image covers the slide; the underlying white fill #FFFFFF is hidden). Black background (#000000) throughout; no gradients, shadows, or boxes. Titles in white #FFFFFF, body statistics in white #FFFFFF with the multiplier/highlight lines in muted gold #C1B485. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans (Medium for titles and gold highlights, Regular for body), a neo-grotesque sans-serif. Main title about 4.4% of slide height, subtitle and body about 2.5%, sub-labels 3.3%, caption about 1.5% in grey #5E5E5E italic. Text is centre-aligned in the middle and right groups.

### Slide 34

**Text**

Vera Rubin NVL144 / Second Half 2026 / 3.6 EF FP4 Inference / 1.2 EF FP8 Training / 3.3X GB300 NVL72 / 13 TB/s HBM4 / 75 TB Fast Memory / 1.6X / 260 TB/s NVLink6 / 2X / 28.8 TB/s CX9 / 2X / Vera / 88 Custom Arm Cores / 176 Threads / 1.8 TB/s NVLink-C2C / Rubin / 2 Reticle-Sized GPUs / 50PF FP4 | 288GB HBM4 / Oberon Rack / Liquid Cooled

**Layout**

Three zones on a black field: a tall full-height rack photograph on the far left (about x 7.6 to 28.6), a centred spec column with the title block at about x 38 to 62, and a right column (about x 70 to 90) holding the Vera and Rubin chip blocks stacked top to bottom. The title sits in the centre column, not at the top, and the left and right columns start near the top and end near the bottom. A small grey caption sits at bottom-left of the centre area beside the rack, with no footer, slide number, logo or date visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph of an Oberon rack (liquid-cooled server rack) at left, tall and narrow, shown full height, boxed against black. The rack is dark charcoal with black cabinet frame and rows of black vents at top and bottom; the middle section holds stacked gold-brass compute trays with cable bundles on both sides. Lighting is soft, studio-style, with specular highlights on the gold trays. Dominant colours: black, charcoal, gold-brass. Framing is straight-on, the rack fills the full slide height. Photograph of a Vera CPU module, a square dark-grey chip package with a central die showing a green-tinted grid of cores and a blue-tinted block, viewed from above on a dark textured background, boxed square with no crop. Photograph of a Rubin GPU package, a rectangular gold-edged package with two large reticle-sized dies in the centre (grey-green and purple-tinted die tiles, with gold heat-spreader segments flanking them), viewed from above on black, boxed with no crop. Several placed image strips sit over the centre spec column at the positions of the text lines; they are not separately legible and appear to match the text lines beneath them.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed rack image on the left and chip photographs placed over it. Full black background, #000000, with no gradient or texture. Title text 'Vera Rubin NVL144' and the 'Vera' and 'Rubin' headings are white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium, a clean geometric sans-serif. Body and subtitle text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Regular. Highlighted multiplier lines (3.3X GB300 NVL72, 1.6X, 2X, 2X) are muted gold #C1B485 in NVIDIASans Medium. The caption is grey #5E5E5E in NVIDIASans Italic. Title text is about 4.4 percent of slide height, body text about 2.5 percent, caption about 1.5 percent. Text is centred. Chip and rack photographs have no borders, shadows or corner radii; the Vera and Rubin chip boxes are sharp-cornered rectangles.

**Layout**

Three zones on a black field: a tall Kyber rack photo boxed at the far left (x 8.7 to 26.8 percent), a centred spec column in the middle (x 37 to 62 percent) headed by the title Rubin Ultra NVL576 at about 29 percent from the top, and a right column (x 70 to 95 percent) holding the Vera block at top and the Rubin Ultra block below. Text in the centre column and right column is centre-aligned. No header bar, footer or slide number; a small italic caption Kyber Rack / Liquid Cooled sits at bottom left of the rack zone at about 89 to 92 percent from the top.

**Visuals**

Three photographs, all rendered on black with no border or tint. (1) Kyber rack: a tall, narrow, vertically boxed photo at x 8.7, y 1.6, 18.1 by 98.4 percent of the slide, showing a liquid-cooled server cabinet in front view, dark grey frame and top and bottom bands, with about four vertical stacks of gold-lit compute blades in dark grey, lit from the front, the cabinet cropped at the bottom edge of the slide. (2) Vera: a square-ish boxed photo of a single processor die at x 76.8, y 15.5, 12.0 by 20.3 percent, dark charcoal package on a black-grey board, with a central bright square die showing a green and blue grid of cores, soft top lighting, dominant colours charcoal, green and blue. (3) Rubin Ultra: a wide horizontal photo of a GPU module at x 71.6, y 58.2, 22.2 by 21.3 percent, a gold-bronze framed rectangular package with four large reticle-sized dies in the middle row, each with grey and gold heat spreaders, lit from above with a soft shadow at the bottom, dominant colours gold, bronze and grey-green. No charts, tables, logos or icons. Raster duplicates of the spec text lines sit over the text positions and are not separately visible.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide white #FFFFFF fill sits underneath, fully covered by a black full-bleed image). Black background, #000000, with no gradient or pattern. Titles and main labels are white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium (a clean geometric sans-serif) at about 4.4 percent of slide height for the headline and 3.3 percent for Vera and Rubin Ultra labels. Body spec lines are NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, at about 2.5 percent of slide height. Highlight multiplier and model lines (14X GB300 NVL72, 8X, 12X, 8X) are Medium weight in muted gold #C1B485. The caption is NVIDIASans Italic in grey #5E5E5E at about 1.5 percent. Photographs have sharp square corners and no shadow. No rules or outline shapes are visible.

**Text**

**Layout**

Centred, symmetrical composition on a black field. The title 'NVIDIA Rubin System' sits centred at the top (about 6.7% from the top). A large circular metallic-sheen image is centred, with a chip illustration in its middle and a caption bar beneath. A thin gold arc runs around the circle with four gold dots on the right and three on the left, each dot marking a stat group; the stat labels are right-aligned in the left column and left-aligned in the right column. A tall server rack image sits at the bottom left, outside the circle. There is no footer, page number or logo, and no margin frame.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a large circular grey-white metallic sheen (diagonal white streaks over black and mid-grey bands, a brushed-chrome look) fills a circle centred on the slide, about 44.5% wide and 79.2% tall, cropped to a circle. In front of it is a top-down rendering of a Vera Rubin GPU package, shown as a dark square die about 30% wide and 50% tall with a grid of small cells; the top and bottom bands are dense fine grids, the left and right edges have tiered connector pads, and the central horizontal band has bright metallic interconnect lines. The cells in the upper-left and centre are lit white-grey, and the right side is darker. Thin gold hairline arc: a ring of about 0.1% stroke around the circle, opening at the top corners and running down to the rack at the bottom left and to the bottom right, passing through seven small gold spheres (about 1% diameter) placed at the left edge (y 43.1%, 52.8%, 63.2%) and the right edge (y 43.1%, 49.8%, 56.5%, 63.2%). Photograph: a tall black server rack or NVLink switch tray, about 3.8% wide and 25.7% tall, with a grid of gold-tinted rows, placed at the bottom left and partly overlapping the gold arc. Caption bar: a dark gradient band behind the caption 'Vera Rubin NVLink576', fading to transparent at its ends. Small transparent overlay images sit behind the stat label groups and are not visually distinct.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed image (the file's white #FFFFFF fill is entirely covered). Pure black background with white and metallic-grey imagery. Title in NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, about 3.3% of slide height, centred. Stat numbers in NVIDIASans Medium, gold #C1B485, about 2.0% of slide height. Stat labels in NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 2.0%. Caption in NVIDIASans Medium, muted gold #8E7D5C, about 2.35%. Sans-serif throughout. Lines: hairline gold arc #C1B485 around 0.1% width. Dots: gold spheres #C1B485 with a light highlight. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on the text; the caption bar has a horizontal dark-to-transparent gradient.

A circular chip photograph sits at the centre, with a tall rack image at lower left and a centred caption band near the bottom. There is no footer, page number or logo visible.

**Visuals**

No chart or table. (1) A thin gold-beige circular arc (about 1 px stroke, #C1B485 at reduced opacity) forms a large ring open at top and bottom, spanning roughly x 24% to 82% of the slide, with seven small gold spheres sitting on it at the label positions: left at y about 43%, 52% and 63%; right at y about 43%, 50%, 57% and 63%. (2) A circular photograph, about 13% of slide width, centred at x 43% to 56%, y 41% to 65%. It shows a top-down view of a Blackwell GPU die: a dark grey rectangular package with a grid of small silver/grey chiplet blocks, a horizontal bar of tiny contacts across the middle, and dark square elements. The surrounding circular field is a mottled silver and grey swirl. It is lit from above, with a monochrome palette and no overlay; the circle is a clean crop. (3) A tall, narrow rack-cabinet photograph at x 28.7% to 34.3%, y 71.5% to 100%. It is a dark black front view of a server rack with gold-lit horizontal bands of trays and a gold top section, cropped by the bottom edge of the slide. (4) A thin horizontal rule pair above and below the caption 'Grace Blackwell NVLink72', forming a dark band, in a faint grey (about #3A3A3A) against black. (5) Small connector and marker artwork images at the label positions, with the gold spheres as their visible parts.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide (a full-bleed black image sits over a white #FFFFFF fill). Black background (#000000) for the whole slide. Title and body in white (#FFFFFF). Numerals and the accent colour are gold-beige #C1B485. Typeface is NVIDIASans, a clean geometric humanist sans-serif: Medium for the title, the numerals and the caption; Regular for the descriptive labels. Title size about 3.3% of slide height (cap height about 2.3%). Body labels about 2.0% of slide height (cap height about 1.4%). Lines are hairline gold arcs. No corner radii, shadows or gradients are visible.

**Layout**

Two bar charts sit side by side in the lower two-thirds of a 16:9 black slide, each with a rounded callout box above its left-hand area and category labels beneath a thin baseline. The title is centred across the top at about 9.5% from the top edge. The footnote sits bottom-right in the margin, with no slide number or logo visible.

**Visuals**

Two vertical bar charts with no printed axis scales, titled only by their callout boxes. Left chart, callout 'Perf = Leadership': Hopper 1, Blackwell 68, Rubin 900; the Rubin bar is the tallest and reaches about 36% from the top, the Blackwell bar about 9% of the slide height at its top, and the Hopper bar is a hairline. Right chart, callout 'TCO / Perf = Cost': Hopper 1, Blackwell 0.13, Rubin 0.03; the Hopper bar is the tallest, about 36% from the top, and the Blackwell and Rubin bars are short, with the Rubin bar a sliver. Each bar is labelled with its value above it in tan #C1B485. Bars are filled with a vertical gradient from light tan #B8A984 at the top to darker bronze #7F7050 at the base. Baselines are thin grey lines #5E5E5E spanning each chart. Both callouts are identical rounded rectangles with a thin tan-grey outline (about #A39A7E, roughly 0.1% of slide width), black interior, and about a 1.5% corner radius. No legend, no highlighted points beyond the gold bars, no table, no photograph.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Spectrum-X “Supercharged” Ethernet
Powering the World’s Most Advanced AI Clouds and Factories
Spectrum-X SuperNIC
Spectrum-X Ethernet Switch
CISCO
CoreWeave
DELL Technologies
Google Cloud
Meta
Microsoft Azure
ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure
xAI

**Layout**

Centred, single-column composition on a full-bleed black slide. The title and subtitle sit centred at the top (about 9.5% to 17% of height). A product hero image spans the middle, with two centred captions beneath it at about 79.5% of height. A row of eight partner logos runs across the bottom at about 88% to 92% of height, spaced evenly from roughly 11% to 91% of width. There is no footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Illustration/photo of two NVIDIA networking products on a black studio background, lit by a soft key light from above, with specular highlights on the metal. Left: a Spectrum-X SuperNIC, a PCIe add-in card lying flat, with a black heatsink on the left end, a small RJ-style port cage, a square ASIC with a green-and-blue iridescent die under a metal frame, a gold PCIe edge connector along the bottom, and two round mounting holes labelled GND. Right: a Spectrum-X Ethernet Switch, a tilted 1U-style chassis with its lid removed, showing rows of black fans at the back, a large dark heatsink over the main processor, a second framed ASIC with green-and-blue die, and a front panel with two rows of QSFP cage ports and gold-coloured handles at both ends. The two products overlap slightly, with the switch behind the NIC. Logos (left to right): Cisco wordmark with bar-graph symbol in white; CoreWeave logo (stylised CW mark plus wordmark) in white; DELL Technologies wordmark in white; Google Cloud wordmark in white; Meta logo (infinity symbol plus wordmark) in white; Microsoft Azure logo (four-square Windows mark plus two-line wordmark) in white; ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure lock-up in white; xAI logo in white. All logos are monochrome white, unboxed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-bleed black image covers the white #FFFFFF base fill). Solid black background with no border or ornament. Title and captions in white #FFFFFF. Subtitle in white #FFFFFF. Logos in white #FFFFFF. Accents come only from the product imagery: gold #C9A45C handles and edge connectors, green #3FAE49 and blue #3B5BDB chip dies. Typeface is NVIDIASans (a custom geometric grotesque, close to a neo-grotesque sans-serif): title in Medium weight, subtitle and captions in Regular or Medium. Title font size is about 3.3% of slide height (cap height about 2.3%), subtitle about 2.2%, captions about 1.7%. Lines are unstyled text; product images have no frames, shadows or corner radii.

fabrinet

LUMENTUM

TFC
tsmc

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a large product photograph occupying the left two-thirds, a text column on the right third, and a row of partner logos along the bottom. The title sits top-right at about 26% from the top, left-aligned at about 67.6% from the left; the captions sit centred under each product image; there is no footer, slide number or visible margin marks.

**Visuals**

Two photographs of NVIDIA silicon photonics packages on a black background, shown as a studio product render. Left: the Spectrum-X Integrated Silicon Photonics package, a large brushed-aluminium/grey-metal square substrate with a dark square chip die in the centre bearing a faint NVIDIA logo, surrounded by gold-tinted rectangular optical connector modules along its top, bottom and left/right edges; the package is viewed at a slight angle with its top-left corner nearest the viewer, lit from above with soft highlights on the metal, and is full-colour but dominated by grey, black and gold. Right, behind and overlapping the lower-right of the Spectrum-X package: the smaller Quantum-X Integrated Silicon Photonics package, a similar grey-metal frame with a dark rectangular die bearing a faint NVIDIA logo and stepped metal mounting lugs with bolt holes, with tall gold-tinted connector strips along its right edge and small connector modules at its bottom. A thin, slightly slanted pale gold-grey vertical line runs from about (54,6) down to about (71,85) with five small gold sphere bullets sitting on it at the left of each bullet text line, the bullets at roughly (67.4,36.7), (67.8,42.8), (69.0,50.1), (69.8,56.0) and (70.2,62.1) as estimates. Logos row (grey-white single-colour wordmarks on black, all white): BROWAVE CORPORATION (Chinese characters with small caps wordmark), COHERENT (wordmark with a circular emblem in the O), CORNING (serif wordmark), fabrinet (bold sans wordmark), FOXCONN with a square H-shaped mark and HON HAI TECHNOLOGY GROUP beneath, LUMENTUM (slash mark and spaced caps), SENKO with Advanced Components beneath, SPIL (block letters), SUMITOMO ELECTRIC (diamond mark with two-line wordmark), TFC (block letters), tsmc (circular logo with small wordmark).

**Design**

Background: Solid black (#000000). Black background (#000000) throughout with white (#FFFFFF) text. Title and bullet text are white (#FFFFFF); caption text is white (#FFFFFF); the accent colour is a metallic gold (#C8A24A) used on the bullet spheres and connector modules, with a grey-metal (#8A8D91) product finish. Typefaces: NVIDIA Sans, a geometric humanist sans-serif; the title is Medium weight, the subtitle and bullets are Regular, the captions are Medium. Title cap height is roughly 4.4% of slide height (font size about 4.4%); subtitle about 2.2%; bullets about 1.85%; captions about 1.7%. Lines are thin, very faint pale grey-gold (#8C7A4F) at about 0.2% stroke width. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on the text; the product photos have soft studio shading.

### Slide 41

**Text**

Electronic IC
Photonic IC
3D Stacked Electronic and Photonic ICs
COUPE uLens with surface coupling
Fiber Connector
Optical Sub-Assembly
External Laser Source Module
Laser Source Package
Co-Packaged Optics Quantum-X Photonic Switch
Interposer

**Layout**

A full-bleed black photographic slide with no title or footer. A horizontal row of five square-cornered thumbnail boxes runs across the top-left, linked left to right by thin grey lines, with the Electronic IC and Photonic IC thumbnails stacked at the far left. Two labelled boxes sit on the right, the External Laser Source Module above the Laser Source Package, with the Interposer label at lower right. Labels sit beside or below their boxes with left alignment. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a black networking switch chassis shown at a three-quarter angle from the front-left, with its front face and gold-coloured ear brackets on the right, pluggable transceivers and cables on top, and two white-silver co-packaged optics modules on the internal board. Lighting is dark and low-key with a soft highlight on the metal. The photo is full-bleed behind everything and fades to black toward the left edge. Five boxed thumbnail micrographs run in a row: an Electronic IC wafer section with blue and green circuit traces; a 3D Stacked Electronic and Photonic ICs cross-section with a pink block; a COUPE uLens with surface coupling shown as a yellow-tinted cross-section; a Fiber Connector showing a green fibre array; and an Optical Sub-Assembly, a silver rectangular package with two fibres. A Photonic IC thumbnail with pink-brown waveguide layout sits below the Electronic IC. Thin light-grey connector lines link the thumbnails and run from the Fiber Connector down to a circle callout on the chassis. A pluggable transceiver is shown near the top centre. The External Laser Source Module is a boxed photo of a black PCB with gold heat sinks. The Laser Source Package is a boxed photo of a silver package with fibres and a green edge board. The Interposer is a boxed photo of a blue PCB with a dense gold-pad array. Two thin white circles mark the co-packaged optics modules on the chassis, and a rectangular label box with a thin white border labels them.

**Design**

Background: Black #000000 full-bleed photograph of the server chassis.

### Slide 42

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph centred on a black field, with no title, body text, footer or slide number. The photograph sits inside a thin rounded-rectangle outline inset about 4% from the left and right edges and about 4.5% from the top and bottom. The switch chassis is horizontally centred and sits in the upper-middle of the frame, while the cables spill out across the lower part and are cropped by the bottom edge of the outline.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a rack-mount network switch seen head-on in a studio. The chassis is brushed champagne-gold metal with a rounded side-handle bar on each end. Along the top row are two rows of black plastic transceiver cages, arranged as three groups of three on each row, nine cages per row, eighteen in total. On the right of the top row are one RJ45 Ethernet port, one small USB port, two RJ45 ports side by side with small network icons beneath them, and one rectangular fibre (SFP-style) port, with tiny printed labels beneath. Below the cages is a dense grid of square ports, each with a yellow-rimmed latch, arranged in about 16 columns and several rows, with small printed numbers above them. To the right is a vertical column of small round vent holes. Yellow fibre-optic patch cables leave the lower ports and fan downward in two bundles, one curving down to the left and one curving down to the right, and they are cropped by the bottom edge of the frame. Lighting is soft and directional from the front, leaving the background black. Dominant colours are black #000000, champagne gold #C9B99A and fibre yellow #E8C21A. The photo is full-bleed within the frame with no tint or overlay, and the cables run off the bottom edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background, with a hidden full-slide white #FFFFFF fill beneath it. Pure black background (#000000) with no gradient or pattern. A thin pale grey (#D9D9D9) rounded-rectangle outline frames the photograph, with a corner radius of about 1.5% of the slide width. No text is present, so no typefaces are used. The photograph carries the entire design.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#FFFFFF]: Full-slide background fill, hidden behind the black backdrop image (Solid fill, no stroke, no shadow, fully covered by the image layers above it.)
- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Black studio backdrop image covering the full slide (Full-bleed, no crop, no overlay, no border.)
- shape at x 3.8, y 4.5, w 92.3, h 91.1 [#000000]: Thin rounded-rectangle frame outline around the photograph (No fill; stroke pale grey #D9D9D9, about 0.2% of slide height wide; corner radius about 1.5% of slide width; no shadow; the photograph is clipped to this rectangle.)
- photo at x 4, y 27, w 92, h 68 [#C9B99A]: Photograph of a rack-mount network switch with transceiver cages, RJ45 and USB ports, a fibre port and yellow fibre-optic patch cables spilling downward (Rectangular crop clipped by the rounded frame outline; the switch chassis spans about x 13 to 87.5 and y 27.5 to 75 of the slide; the cables run from about y 45 down to the bottom edge of the frame and overlap the lower chassis ports; no tint or overlay; soft studio lighting.)

A horizontal timeline across the full width: three generation panels (Blackwell, Rubin, Feynman) are rounded boxes that step up in height from left to right and overlap at their edges, with a row-label column at the far left (COMPUTE, NVLINK (SCALE-UP), NETWORKING (SCALE-OUT), SYSTEM) right-aligned at about x 8 to 25 percent. The title is centred at the top (y 9.5 percent) with a centred subtitle line beneath it; generation names sit above their panels, and years 2025 to 2028 are centred in a row at the bottom (y 92.5 percent) under a thin arrowed baseline. No footer, logo or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram/grid of product chips on a black field. Blackwell panel (x 14.0 to 37.4, y 24.1 to 79.6 percent) contains: Blackwell 8S HBM3e GPU package (gold-framed die, top-left), Blackwell Ultra 8S HBM3e package (gold-framed die, right of it), Grace CPU (dark square package with green-and-pink die), 5th Gen NVL 72 NVLink switch chip (gold square package), Spectrum5 51T switch (silver-gold package), CX8 800G NIC (gold package). Rubin panel (x 37.4 to 62.0, y 23.2 to 80.7 percent) contains: Rubin 8S HBM4 package, Rubin Ultra 16S HBM4e package (wide gold module), Vera CPU (dark square package), 6th Gen NVSwitch 3600 GB/s (gold package), 7th Gen NVSwitch 3600 GB/s (gold package), Spectrum6 102T, CPO (silver package), CX9 1600G (gold package). Feynman panel (x 62.0 to 88.4, y 22.1 to 82.0 percent) contains: Feynman Next-Gen HBM package (hand-sketched white-outline square with yellow-gold fill), Vera CPU (dark square package), 8th Gen NVSwitch NVL-Next (sketched white-outline nested squares with gold fill), Spectrum7 204T, CPO (sketched package with gold fill), CX10 (sketched white-outline nested squares with gold fill). Rack illustrations: Oberon NVL72 Liquid Cooled rack (x 30.0 to 34.9, y 53.1 to 86.7 percent), a tall black cabinet with gold horizontal compute trays and a lighter top manifold, lit from the front; Kyber NVL576 Liquid Cooled rack (x 54.9 to 59.8, y 53.1 to 86.7 percent), a tall black cabinet densely filled with vertical gold-lit rails. Timeline arrow: thin white horizontal line from x 8 to 89 at y 90.1 percent with arrowhead at the right end.

Background: Solid black #000000 (full-bleed photographic background image, black). Black background (#000000) with no gradient. Title white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIA Sans Medium, about 3.3 percent of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 2.2 percent. Panels: dark charcoal fill (about #111111) with thin light-grey (about #D0D0D0) rounded outline, corner radius about 1 percent of slide width, no shadow. Chips are gold-framed squares with no shadow.

### Slide 45

**Text**

Reinventing $500B Enterprise IT For the Age of AI
AI
OS
Query
KV $
Prefill
Decode
INFRASTRUCTURE
Compute
Networking
Storage

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with no visible margins or footer. The title is centred horizontally in the top band. Three rows sit below it: AI row of three boxed diagrams, OS row with a stacked-card diagram centred, and an INFRASTRUCTURE row of three sketches with captions. Row labels AI, OS and INFRASTRUCTURE are left-aligned in the left column. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Three framed AI architecture diagrams in the AI row, each a dark rounded rectangle with thin grey outline containing small multicolour flowchart icons (green, orange, red, purple) joined by thin grey connector lines; labels inside are too small to transcribe reliably. Left diagram shows a Query/Audio/Tool Call flow into a Long Reasoning Agent box listing green-icon steps and LLM model boxes. Middle diagram shows a Retrieval Pipeline and Data Processing section with Vector RAG and Graph RAG blocks. Right diagram shows a user-to-LLM pipeline with an Ingestion Pipeline band. In the OS row, a stack of five offset dark rounded cards with thin grey edges; the front card holds a left-to-right flow: a Query input arrow into a Prefill block (gold grid chip icon with bronze frame), an arrow to a gold rectangle labelled KV $, an arrow to a Decode block (gold grid chip icon), then a row of five small squares (first white, four dark grey) with a loop arrow from Decode back to Prefill and an outgoing arrow to the right. In the INFRASTRUCTURE row, a hand-sketched white line drawing of a tall rack server with gold-filled slots labelled Compute; a hand-sketched white line drawing of a flat networking switch with a central chip square labelled Networking; a hand-sketched white line drawing of a storage chassis with grid drive bays labelled Storage. All sketches are white outlines on black with gold fills in the compute rack.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, from a full-slide image layer. Black background (#000000). Title in white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Medium sans-serif, roughly 3.3 percent of slide height. Row labels AI, OS and INFRASTRUCTURE in tan gold (#C1B485), NVIDIASans Medium, about 2.2 percent of slide height. Captions Compute, Networking and Storage in white NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.2 percent. Diagram accents are gold (#C1B485 to bronze) and the frames are dark grey (#1A1A1A) with thin grey (#3A3A3A) outlines. Corner radii are small on the framed diagrams and on the OS cards. No shadows or gradients.
