### Slide 46

**Text**

NVIDIA

**Layout**

Single full-bleed black field with one product photograph centred horizontally and sitting slightly below the vertical middle; the product runs about 15% to 87% across and 31% to 68% down. No title, body text, footer, slide number or margins are present beyond the photograph's own frame.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a horizontal rack-mount server chassis (about 2U) shown from the front-left at a low three-quarter angle, cropped by nothing and sitting on a pure black backdrop with no visible floor or reflection. The left end is a dark black perforated panel with two small USB-style ports and a rectangular latch. Next is a narrow bronze-gold vertical edge strip with a round bronze latch at its bottom. The main body is a bronze-gold, sandblasted or foam-like textured front panel with two recessed oval pull handles: a large one on the left with a vertical NVIDIA logo and vertical NVIDIA wordmark, and a smaller one on the right. The lighting is a soft key light from above and the left, producing highlights on the metal edges and handles. Dominant colours are black #000000 and bronze-gold #A08A5A, with a lime-green logo mark #76B900. The product is not boxed, tinted or overlaid.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 photographic backdrop, covering the full slide. Black photographic backdrop with no text or graphic design layer. The only colours are the black background, bronze-gold metal and the lime-green NVIDIA mark. No typefaces apart from the vertical NVIDIA wordmark, which is a bold geometric sans-serif in capitals in dark bronze #5A4E36 on the panel. No corner radii, gradients or shadows beyond the photographic lighting.

### Slide 47

**Text**

Fine-tuning DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B / On DGX Spark using the NV-Code dataset / Installation and Setup / Model Setup / Welcome to NVIDIA DGX Spark / Let's set up your new personal AI supercomputer (tiny, partly illegible) / Get started (button, partly illegible). No text is printed on the slide itself; the lines above are legible only on the laptop screen inside the photograph.

**Layout**

A single photograph occupies the slide, centred, with a black surround of about 2.7% on the left and right and about 3.3% at top and bottom. The photo sits in a thin gold-bronze hairline frame. There is no title, footer, slide number or logo, and no text outside the photograph.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a wooden desk in a modern office. In the centre is an open silver laptop (MacBook-style, about 16 inch) with its screen facing away from the viewer towards the camera's point of view: the screen shows a dark code editor (left, with a notebook titled Fine-tuning DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B, Python code in colour) and, in front of it, a smaller dark browser window showing a 'Welcome to NVIDIA DGX Spark' page with a rendered grey-bronze DGX Spark box above a green button. The laptop dock row is visible along the bottom of the screen. On the right, a bronze-gold, textured, rectangular DGX Spark desktop AI computer sits on the desk with brushed metal end caps. On the left, a pair of dark tortoiseshell-frame eyeglasses lies on the desk, beside a stack of papers, a dark box and a teal folder. The background is a defocused office with a horizontal ceiling light strip at top left, dark pillars, grey walls and a bright window with blurred greenery at the right. Lighting is soft, warm and diffuse from the window and ceiling. Dominant colours: warm wood brown #8A6A4F, slate grey #5E6168, black #000000, bronze #B8A27A, teal #2F7F7A and white screen glow. The photo is full, not cropped; it is boxed inside a thin bronze hairline. No overlay or tint is visible beyond a faint vignette.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 surround behind a full-colour photograph; a white #FFFFFF fill sits underneath the full slide. Pure black surround #000000 framing a full-colour photograph. No titles, body text or accent typography on the slide. The only typography is the tiny on-screen UI text within the photo, in a sans-serif (SF-style) regular weight. Thin bronze-gold hairline frame around the photo, about #B8A27A, roughly 0.2% of slide height wide. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients other than the natural lighting in the photo.

Two-zone layout on a black slide with no margins or footer. A large product photograph fills the left half, running from about 4% to 57% of the width and from 3% to 98.6% of the height. The title block sits in the upper-middle of the right column, the four gold-dot bullets follow in a vertical list, and a horizontal row of partner logos sits near 73% of the height. There is no visible page number or footer, and the title is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Photograph: an open-frame NVIDIA DGX Station workstation chassis seen from the side in a dark, low-key studio render. The black metal case has a large side window or open panel on the right showing three stacked fan modules with hexagonal honeycomb grilles, each about 20% of the slide height. The left part of the interior shows a motherboard with a large gold-framed GB300 superchip module (a dark grey package with a multicolour die and gold rim) in the upper centre, a smaller grey square chip labelled NVIDIA at the upper left, a second smaller chip with a multicoloured iridescent die in the lower-left centre, PCIe slots in the lower left, memory slots and gold-ringed mounting standoffs throughout, and a rear I/O cluster with stacked ports at the far left edge. The photo is not cropped on its right or left edges, and sits unboxed directly on the black background with no overlay or tint. Lighting is a soft directional key from the upper left, with gold and warm highlights on the metal parts and deep shadows. Dominant colours are black #000000 and dark grey #1A1A1A, with gold #C1B485 accents. Graphic: four gold sphere bullets (about 0.9% wide, 1.6% high) at x 61.3%, at heights of about 44%, 51%, 58% and 64%, each with a gold-to-brown radial gradient and a vertical gold hairline connecting them. Logos: ASUS wordmark in white, BOXX wordmark in white, DELL Technologies wordmark in white, the hp roundel logo in white, a Lambda wordmark with a lambda glyph in a square outline in white, and the SUPERMICRO oval logo in white at the far right. Each logo is about 2 to 3.5% of the slide width.

**Design**

Background is solid black #000000 with no gradient or texture. Title 'DGX Station' is set in NVIDIASans Medium (close to a neo-grotesque sans-serif) in white #FFFFFF, at about 4.4% of slide height. Subtitle 'The Ultimate Workstation / for AI and Data Science' is NVIDIASans Regular in white #FFFFFF, about 2.2% of slide height, with a tight two-line leading. Bullet labels are NVIDIASans Medium in gold #C1B485 for the key figures ('GB300', '784 GB', '20,000', 'ConnectX-8') followed by NVIDIASans Regular in white #FFFFFF for the rest of each line, about 1.85% of slide height. Accents are gold #C1B485. Shapes have no corner radii, shadows or outlines; the hairline is a thin gold #8A7A4F rule about 1 px wide. No gradients on the background; the gold spheres carry a soft metallic gradient.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a single centred title across the top at about 8.4% from the top. Below it, six NVIDIA AI systems are arranged left to right in a single row of ascending size, sitting on a common baseline near the bottom, with a label beneath each product. Specification figures in gold sit under the name labels. No footer, slide number or logo is shown; the margins are wide and the composition is horizontally centred.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style product render, not a chart, composited on black, with six black NVIDIA systems in a left-to-right size progression. (1) DGX Spark: a small gold-and-black desktop box lying flat near the bottom left, labelled DGX Spark, 1 PFLOPS. (2) RTX PRO Workstation: an open-side tower with a black chassis and visible gold GPU cards, labelled RTX PRO Workstation. (3) DGX Station: a tall open-side workstation tower with a gold GPU module visible inside, labelled DGX Station, 20 PFLOPS. (4) RTX PRO Enterprise Server: a tall closed black rack with stacked server trays and green status lights, labelled RTX PRO Enterprise Server. (5) DGX B200: a black two-bay rack holding four large gold-tinted modules with NVIDIA logos, labelled DGX B200. (6) DGX GB300: a wide black rack of four bays with gold compute trays and copper-coloured cabling, labelled DGX GB300, 15 EFLOPS. Lighting is soft and directional from the front-left, producing gold highlights on the hardware. Dominant colours are black, with gold #C1B485 and muted bronze metal. Each system is shown full-frame within the photo, not cropped, and no overlay or tint is applied.

**Design**

Background is solid black with the product photograph sitting directly on it. Title is NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, a sans-serif with medium weight, about 3.3% of slide height, centred. Product labels are NVIDIASans Regular in white #FFFFFF at about 1.7% of slide height, centred under each product. Spec figures are NVIDIASans Medium in gold #C1B485 at about 2.0% of slide height. No rules, shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients are present.

**Layout**

Black slide with a left-hand flow diagram column (x about 18 to 40), a centred-right title block at top (x about 44 to 75, y about 16 to 23), a two-row logo grid (x about 40 to 81, y about 31 to 42) and a stacked hardware illustration with right-aligned labels in the lower right (x about 51 to 81, y about 46 to 91). A bracket line joins the diagram to the hardware stack. No footer, date or slide number is visible; margins are about 18 percent on the left and 19 percent on the right.

**Visuals**

Left: a circular green-outlined avatar (3D cartoon figure of a man with grey hair, dark-framed glasses and a black top, front-facing, centred in the ring) inside a thin bright green (#76B900) circle with soft green glow, about 20 percent of slide width in diameter. Below it a hexagonal cube icon with a white cloud symbol and green base (Llama Nemotron) connects by a double-headed grey arrow to a second hexagonal cube icon with a green network symbol (NeMo Retriever). Two grey arrows rise from below: from an orange-outlined document-and-image icon (Enterprise Files, orange #E8601C-ish outline on black) and from an orange cylinder database icon with a radiation-style badge (Vector Database, NVIDIA cuVS), elbow connectors with rounded corners. A thin grey bracket line runs from the right side of the diagram column to a horizontal line that reaches the hardware stack. Right: a photographic-style exploded view of stacked NVIDIA server units on a black background, lit from above with soft highlights, showing from top: a small gold-and-black compute module with fan vents, a grey-silver 1U compute chassis with a green-accented board, a long black networking switch with a row of ports and gold-coloured handles, and a dark storage chassis at the bottom with a grid of drive bays and a black ConnectX card. Logos: row 1 (white): ddn (white circle and lowercase wordmark), DELL Technologies (white wordmark), Hewlett Packard Enterprise (white rectangle outline above stacked wordmark), Hitachi Vantara (white target emblem with bold wordmark), IBM (white striped wordmark). Row 2 (white): NetApp (white square emblem with wordmark), NUTANIX (white wordmark), PURESTORAGE (white hexagon emblem with wordmark and registered mark), VAST (white inverted triangle emblem with wordmark), WEKA (white angular emblem with wordmark). No photographs apart from the avatar and hardware.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, with a full-slide white #FFFFFF fill beneath it that the black image covers. Solid black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title and labels, bright green (#76B900) for NVIDIA product names and darker green (#60A500) for Llama Nemotron and NeMo Retriever labels, orange (#E8601C) accents on the Enterprise Files and Vector Database icons. Typeface NVIDIA Sans, a geometric-humanist sans-serif. Title in Medium weight, body labels in Regular weight. Title text about 3.3 percent of slide height (about 37 pt on a 7.5 in slide), labels about 1.5 percent (about 17 pt), cuVS label about 1.2 percent. Thin grey (#8C8C8C) line strokes about 1 px wide, square-cornered text, rounded corners on the elbow connectors, glow around the avatar ring, no gradients or drop shadows apart from the avatar glow.

### Slide 51

**Text**

DELL Technologies / + / NVIDIA / INDUSTRY'S BROADEST END-TO-END / NVIDIA AI ENTERPRISE INFRASTRUCTURE / AI PCs / Spark / Station / Compute / Networking / Storage / Blackwell Compute Racks / 2,000+ Customers / 100+ New releases / 49+ Exaflops

**Layout**

Stage photograph fills the slide inside a thin black frame with rounded corners. A full-width dark-navy panel sits in the upper-middle band. Its top row holds the Dell Technologies and NVIDIA logos at left with a centred two-line headline, and a row of six products with labels sits below. A thin horizontal rule separates this from a band of three statistic boxes in a row, left-aligned to the panel margins. A tall Blackwell rack stands at the right edge of the header row. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a dark conference stage shown behind a navy graphic panel. The stage is lit by small white spotlights along the top and bottom edges, with purple and violet side beams. The stage floor is a dark platform with a purple-lit edge. Upright and boxed items on the platform include a tall purple-lit server rack at about centre-left (overlapping the 2,000+ box), two small purple plinths with a black server unit on top near centre, and a dark audience silhouette in the lower foreground. The photograph is full-bleed behind the frame and tinted by the navy panel. Product images on the panel, left to right: an AI PC laptop with a dark screen and blue glow; a small black NVIDIA DGX Spark unit; a black tower workstation with a green stripe (Station); a Dell rack compute chassis with multiple server blades (Compute); a grey network switch with rows of ports and SFP cages (Networking); a Dell storage enclosure with a hexagonal-mesh front (Storage); a tall silver and black Blackwell compute rack. Logos: Dell Technologies wordmark in white; a white plus sign; NVIDIA eye logo in green with black NVIDIA wordmark. Dominant colours: navy blue, black, white, green and gold. Statistic numerals are cream-gold with white labels.

**Design**

Background: Photograph of a dark stage with a black frame; the content area is a solid navy panel #0B1F5C. Dark stage background with black frame and navy panel #0B1F5C. Headline and labels in white #FFFFFF. Statistic numerals in gold-cream #F2D49B; statistic labels in white #FFFFFF. Accent rule and box outlines in mid blue #3A63B8. NVIDIA logo green #76B900. Headline typeface is a light-weight neo-grotesque sans-serif (Helvetica-like), uppercase, with text size about 4.6% of slide height. Labels are regular-weight sans-serif at about 2.2% of slide height. Statistic numerals are light-weight sans-serif at about 8% of slide height. Stat boxes have thin blue outlines with slightly rounded corners (about 1% radius) and no fill. The navy panel has no shadow and a flat fill.

**Layout**

A large rounded dark panel fills the centre of the slide, holding a scatter chart with its y-axis at the left and x-axis along the bottom. Three centred icons with labels sit in a row below the panel. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Scatter chart in a rounded dark panel. Y-axis titled 'Tokens/s', labelled 0, 1,000, 2,000, 3,000 (linear, range 0 to about 3,400, gridlines absent). X-axis titled 'Average Accuracy Across Agentic Tasks (%)', labelled 50, 55, 60, 65, 70, 75, 80 (range 50 to 80). No legend. Below the chart, three icons of equal size: each is a dark charcoal hexagonal cube with a white thought-cloud symbol and a bright NVIDIA green (#76B900 range) base slab, labelled Nano, Super and Ultra in white.

Background: Solid black #000000 background.

**Layout**

A logo sits on the left third of a black slide, vertically near the middle. A large rounded-corner bordered panel occupies the right of the slide, holding the architecture diagram titled Accenture AI Refinery at its top left. No visible footer, page number or margin text.

**Visuals**

Logo: the accenture wordmark in white lowercase sans-serif with a purple chevron (>) above the 'e' of 'accenture'. Diagram: a dark architecture diagram with grey-lavender boxes and purple section headers. Knowledge block (top left): Data Extraction arrow down to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever (green NVIDIA icon), which connects to Synthetic DSC, Industry Data Products and Semantic Layer boxes; arrows go to Vector DB and Search & Response icons. Agent block (top centre): a vertical Distiller bar on the left, Session Creation bar, SQL Database icon, Session Memory, Orchestrator box containing Planning, Super and Tooling boxes and NVIDIA Blueprint icon, and a Vector Database icon connected below with double arrows; KeyVault Service and Distributed Cache Server icons sit above. Governance & Observability block (top right): Usage Tracker, FinOps and Dashboard icons joined by arrows, with Usage Data Infra, Token, Agent box below and Application/Guardrails/Model Management and Deployment & Ops boxes to the right. Physical AI block: NVIDIA Omniverse icon, USD box and NVIDIA Blueprint icon. Far right: vertical AI Refinery SDK bar beside Super Agent Customized, Utility Agent RAG and Utility Agent Industry Expert robot icons. Inference block: Model Registry row with NVIDIA NIM, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever and Llama Nemotron Reason; Container Registry row with NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails, NVIDIA RIVA and Custom Container (Docker) icons; Online Inference column with Load Balancer, Ingress Controller and Prompt Routing boxes. Training block: Data Storage and Model Registry boxes feed arrows to NVIDIA NeMo Curator, NVIDIA NeMo and NVIDIA NeMo Evaluator boxes, which connect to a vertical Database bar. Infrastructure block (bottom): NVIDIA GPU chip icon, DGX Cloud icon with GPU and Kubernetes-style icons, and K8s Control Plane box with Controller Manager, API-Server and Scheduler boxes linked by arrows. Purple double arrows link blocks. Colours: purple #A100FF accents, lavender-grey #9A93A8 boxes, dark grey #3A3A3A outlines, NVIDIA green #76B900 icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photograph-free background (full-slide image of black). Black background #000000 with a white-outlined rounded panel (thin light grey #BBBBBB stroke, corner radius about 2% of slide width) for the diagram. Title text in white #FFFFFF, bold geometric sans-serif (Graphik-like), about 3% of slide height. Diagram labels in white #FFFFFF and light grey #D0D0D0 sans-serif, about 1% of slide height. Accent purple #A100FF for the logo chevron and section headers. Section header bars are purple #7F00C8 with white text. Boxes are lavender-grey #9A93A8 with thin outlines; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 55

**Text**

AT&T / Ask AT&T Gen AI Platform / AT&T Knowledge Base / AT&T KDA / AT&T Docs / AT&T Data / AT&T Domain Services / NVIDIA NeMo Curator / User Interface / Kubeflow Training Operator / Volcano Scheduler / Orchestrator / NVIDIA NeMo Customizer / Training Job K8s GPU Cluster / DataStore / NVIDIA NeMo Evaluator / NVIDIA LLM NIM / NVIDIA Triton Inference Server / AT&T Inference Platform / End User Application

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a black slide: the AT&T logo sits at the left, vertically centred at about 42 to 57% of the slide height, and a large rounded-corner bordered panel holding the platform flow diagram occupies the right side from about 38% to 95% horizontally and 21% to 79% vertically. The diagram title sits at the top left inside the panel. There is no visible footer, slide number or source line.

**Visuals**

Two raster images. (1) AT&T globe-and-wordmark logo in bright blue #1A9BD7 globe with white AT&T wordmark, placed at the left. (2) A flow diagram of dark rectangles with thin blue and green outlines on black, with blue elbow arrows connecting boxes. Left group: a rounded dark-blue outlined container labelled AT&T Domain Services (with small AT&T globe icon) holding four stacked boxes AT&T Knowledge Base, AT&T KDA, AT&T Docs, AT&T Data; an arrow leaves the container's right edge into NVIDIA NeMo Curator (dark olive box with green NVIDIA cube icon). Curator has an arrow down to DataStore (dark box with blue server icon) and an arrow right to a top-centre User Interface box (green label, small colour icon) with a downward arrow to Orchestrator (blue-grey box with cloud icon). Orchestrator has arrows right to Kubeflow Training Operator (blue box with blue icon) and to NVIDIA NeMo Customizer (olive box with green cube icon). Kubeflow and Customizer connect to Volcano Scheduler (dark box with red-orange label and icon); Volcano has a downward arrow to Training Job K8s GPU Cluster (dark blue box), which has a downward arrow to NVIDIA NeMo Evaluator (olive box with green cube icon). Evaluator has a leftward arrow into DataStore. DataStore has a downward arrow into a large dark-blue outlined container labelled AT&T Inference Platform (with small AT&T globe icon) containing NVIDIA LLM NIM (olive box with green cube icon) and NVIDIA Triton Inference Server (dark box with green hexagon gear icon). A rightward arrow leaves the container into End User Application (dark box with green label). Dominant colours: black, blue #1A9BD7 lines and labels, NVIDIA green #76B900 icons and accents, olive #2B3A12 box fills.

**Design**

### Slide 58

**Text**

Capital One MACAW / Synthetic Data Generation / Prompts / NVIDIA Triton Inference Server / Synthetic Data / NVIDIA TensorRT / NVIDIA A100 / Customization / NVIDIA NeMo / PyTorch / Kubernetes / Customized LLM / Herd / Evaluation / NVIDIA NeMo / NVIDIA Triton Inference Server / Kubernetes / Open Weight / LLMs / Capital One / Customization / Optimization / Real-time / Inference / Customized LLMs / Optimization / NVIDIA TensorRT / Compilation / FP8 / EAGLE / Optimized LLM / Herd / User / Prompt / NeMo Guardrails / MACAW / Understander / Planner / Evaluator / Explainer / Optimized LLM Herd / SLM / NVIDIA A10G / NVIDIA NeMo / NVIDIA TensorRT / Kubernetes / NVIDIA H100

**Layout**

Two-zone horizontal layout on a full black slide: the Capital One logo sits alone at left-centre (about x 7 to 30 percent, vertically centred around y 42 to 57 percent), and a single framed diagram panel occupies the right side from about x 38 to 95 percent and y 21 to 79 percent. Inside the panel the title Capital One MACAW sits top-left, and a narrow column of three dark-teal row labels (Customization, Optimization, Real-time Inference) runs down the left, with flow boxes arranged in three horizontal rows to their right. There is no footer, no page number and no slide-level title outside the panel.

**Visuals**

Two raster images and one embedded diagram. (1) Capital One logo: a white bold italic wordmark 'CapitalOne' with a red swoosh arc passing above and below it, placed on black, at about x 6.9 y 41.6 w 23.1 h 14.7 percent. (2) Architecture diagram (a full-colour-on-black flow chart, 2295 by 1292 px image) at about x 37.8 y 21.3 w 57.4 h 57.4 percent, drawn as a rounded rectangle with a thin light-grey outline. Diagram composition: three horizontal bands labelled by teal boxes on the left. Band 1 (Synthetic Data Generation, then Customization, then Evaluation): Prompts (olive-yellow database cylinder icon) points right to NVIDIA Triton Inference Server (green gear-hexagon icon) which points right to Synthetic Data (olive database cylinder); below Triton, NVIDIA TensorRT (green gear-hexagon) with a dashed curve from Open Weight LLMs (blue brain icon) rising to the right to the Optimization group; a grey-outlined NVIDIA A100 GPU card icon (green) sits on the connector between the Synthetic Data group and the Customization group. Customization group: NVIDIA NeMo (green hierarchy/gear icon) above a magenta-and-black PyTorch logo tile above Kubernetes (blue ship-wheel icon); an arrow leads to a small red-and-grey brain icon labelled Customized LLM Herd, then to the Evaluation group containing NVIDIA NeMo (green hierarchy icon), NVIDIA Triton Inference Server (green gear icon) and Kubernetes (blue wheel icon). Band 2: Customized LLMs (red brain icon) points right to the Optimization group containing NVIDIA TensorRT (green gear icon) with the list Compilation, FP8, EAGLE to its right; an arrow leads to Optimized LLM Herd (a pair of grey-green brain icons). Band 3: a User icon (white outline person in a circle) points right to a grey rounded box labelled Prompt, which points to NeMo Guardrails (a dark grey hexagonal cube with a green emblem); two arrows leave Guardrails to the right into the MACAW group, which holds four boxes in a row: Understander (purple fill, white text), Planner (magenta fill, white text), Evaluator (black fill, white outline, white text), Explainer (grey fill, white text); dashed curved arrows run from Understander and Planner and from Evaluator and Explainer down to a small Optimized LLM Herd pair of green brain icons; a small brain icon labelled SLM sits left of the bottom bar, with a dashed line from it to the infrastructure bar. Infrastructure bar along the bottom of the MACAW band (rounded outline box): NVIDIA A10G (green GPU card), NVIDIA NeMo (green hierarchy icon), NVIDIA TensorRT (green gear icon), Kubernetes (blue wheel icon), NVIDIA H100 (green GPU card); a dashed vertical line rises from the H100 position to the MACAW group. Lighting: flat, no photographic lighting. Nothing is cropped, the logo and diagram are not full-bleed, and no tint or overlay is applied.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background across the whole slide (a white #FFFFFF fill sits beneath the full-slide image, which is black). Dark-mode technical diagram on pure black. The title Capital One MACAW is bold white sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial-like), about 2.3 percent of slide height. Labels are white or light grey regular sans-serif, about 1.0 to 1.2 percent of slide height. Row-label boxes are dark teal #0A3D5C with thin lighter teal borders #1E6E8C and white bold text. Group containers have thin dark-green rounded outlines #2F4F1F with dark olive-green dividers and group titles in white. Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900 for gear and hierarchy icons, olive-yellow #C8B400 for database cylinders, blue #2D6BD8 for Kubernetes and Open Weight brain icons, red #C8102E for the Capital One swoosh and the red-tinted LLM brain icons, purple #7A2DB5 for Understander, magenta #A8195A for Planner, grey #6E6E6E for Explainer and Prompt #808080. Connectors are thin light grey #BDBDBD arrows, some dashed. Diagram frame: rounded corners about 1 percent of width, thin light grey #D0D0D0 stroke, no shadow.

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: the Deloitte logo sits alone on the left third, vertically centred at about 46% from the top and roughly 9% from the left edge. A large rounded-corner frame holding the Deloitte Zora AI architecture diagram occupies the right 57% of the slide, starting at about 38% from the left and spanning 21% to 79% of the height. No title placeholder, footer, page number or margins are otherwise visible on the slide.

**Visuals**

One architecture diagram inside a rounded-corner outlined frame. Shapes: green rectangles (#3A7D22) for Deloitte Models on NIM, Model and Prompt Management, Business Analytics Agent, Final Generation Agent, Data Retrieval Agent, Tabular Query Agent, Routine Generation Agent, and Data Pre-processing items Chunking, Processing, Indexing; grey panels for RAG Knowledge Storage, Data Pre-processing for RAG, and the Logs bar with boxes User Feedback, Application Logs, Model and KPI Logs, ......, Other Logs; vertical grey pill labels Model Endpoints and Finetuned Model; a tall grey bar Authentication/Authorization; a navy Enterprise Core panel containing a blue Generative AI Hub box with ERP AI Core sub-boxes and a Zora APP bar, a light grey column with blue buttons SAC, Chatbot, UIS/UX Apps and a grey UX Apps box; a blue Enterprise Data box. Icons: NVIDIA NIM hexagon icons above several agents, NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails shield icon in a green-outlined rounded box, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever, NVIDIA NeMo Curator, NVIDIA NeMo LLM Pretraining / Fine-Tuning and NVIDIA NeMo Evaluation & Optimization icons, Llama 3 logo, Llama Nemotron Reason icon, SQL database icon beside CSP Database, Vector DB (Milvus) green cylinder, Data Backup grey cylinder, Vector vector engine hexagon icon. Arrows: double-headed arrow from Guardrails to Final Generation Agent NIM; single arrows up from Routine Generation Agent to Tabular Query Agent, to Data Retrieval Agent, to Final Generation Agent; arrows from Data Pre-processing to RAG Knowledge Storage; arrows from NeMo Curator up to Data Ingestion; double-headed arrows linking the Authentication/Authorization bar to agents and to Zora APP; a line from Logs to Enterprise Data. Dominant colours: black, green, grey, navy and bright blue.

### Slide 60

**Text**

EY.ai Agent Platform / Ingestion Pipeline / Contract Data Retrieval Pipeline / Contracts / (Blob Storage) / Metadata / Hierarchy / Entities / OCR / NVIDIA / Blueprint / RAG / Vector / Database and / Indexer / Milvus / Backend / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Feedback/Metadata / Relevant / Context / User query + / Relevant context / NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails / Llama Nemotron Reason / FastAPI / User Interface / Query against / Contracts / User / Application / Front-end / Query / Input / Output / NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails / NVIDIA NIM / Guardrailed response / Content Safety, / Hallucination / LLM Response / LLM Response + / Output Parser / NVIDIA NIM / Citations

**Layout**

Two-zone composition on a full black slide: an EY logo sits at left-centre (about x 15 to 22 %, y 41 to 54 %) and a single framed architecture diagram occupies the right side (x 37.8 to 95.2 %, y 21.3 to 78.8 %). No title sits above the slide; the diagram's own heading 'EY.ai Agent Platform' is at its top-left inside the frame. A small EY logo sits at the top-right corner of the frame (x 88 to 91 %, y 28 to 34 %). No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram (NVIDIA-style reference diagram for the EY.ai Agent Platform) drawn in thin grey and white outlines on black, with yellow and NVIDIA-green accents. Outer container: a rounded rectangle with a thin light-grey (#BFBFBF) outline, about 0.5 % stroke, corner radius about 1.5 % of slide width, no fill. Yellow vertical bar (#FFE600) at x 41.9 %, y 28 to 75 %, about 0.9 % wide, spanning the left side of the panel. Group 'Ingestion Pipeline': a rectangle with thin yellow-olive outline (#8A8A00-ish) containing the text 'Ingestion Pipeline'; inside it, a dashed box 'Contract Data Retrieval Pipeline' containing a solid-outlined sub-box with bullets Metadata, Hierarchy, Entities, OCR; an icon labelled 'Contracts (Blob Storage)' in a dashed box with a yellow pulse-and-magnifier glyph; arrow from Contracts to the bullet box; NVIDIA Blueprint green-and-grey 3D cube icon; arrow to RAG dashed box containing a grey database cylinder icon labelled 'Vector Database and Indexer' and the word 'Milvus' in bold below; a small white square at top-right of RAG with a blue eye icon. Arrows from RAG to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever (rightward, solid light grey) and back (leftward, from Retriever to Milvus). Group 'Backend' (plain label above a dashed box): NVIDIA NeMo Retriever dashed box with green-and-grey cube icon; arrow to a grey box labelled 'Relevant Context'; grey database icon labelled 'Feedback/Metadata' above it. Below the Retriever: two arrows down and up to Llama Nemotron Reason dashed box with cloud-shaped green icon; NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails cube icon above with label and arrow rightward to Llama Nemotron Reason; FastAPI dashed box with a teal-green icon and label; lower NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails cube icon with label; NVIDIA NIM icon in a dashed box (network-node icon, green); Content Safety, Hallucination dashed box; LLM Response + Output Parser dashed box; another NVIDIA NIM icon at right in a dashed box. Group 'User Interface': a rectangle with thin yellow-olive outline containing a yellow user silhouette icon labelled 'User', a grey laptop icon labelled 'Application Front-end', and two light-grey rectangles labelled 'Input' and 'Output' under the caption 'Query'. Arrows: User to laptop (labelled 'Query against Contracts'); Input to FastAPI (elbow arrow up); Output receives arrow from Content Safety labelled 'Guardrailed response'; LLM Response arrow leftward labelled 'LLM Response'; from 'Relevant Context' a line runs down to LLM Response + Output Parser labelled 'User query + Relevant context'; a long elbow line runs from Relevant Context down and left along the bottom to the Output box, labelled 'Citations' in yellow. Icons: NVIDIA logo-style cubes with green (#76B900) bases and grey-white glyphs, EY logo (white wordmark with yellow swoosh) at left and top-right; Milvus icon (grey database cylinders) and eye icon (blue #1CA7E0 on white). Lighting: flat, no shading; no photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed image (the slide-wide image covers 0,0 to 100,100 %; a white #FFFFFF fill lies beneath it and is fully covered). Pure black background (#000000) for the whole slide, with the diagram on black. Text is white (#FFFFFF) for the title and labels, with bold weights on 'Contract', 'NVIDIA Blueprint', 'Milvus' and 'NVIDIA NeMo Retriever'. Accents: EY yellow #FFE600 for the vertical bar, the EY swoosh, 'User Interface' and 'Ingestion Pipeline' outlines, and 'Citations'; NVIDIA green #76B900 for icon bases. Grey boxes (#B0B0B0 fill with black text) for Relevant Context, Input and Output. Typeface: diagram labels in a neo-grotesque sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial-like), regular weight; the diagram heading 'EY.ai Agent Platform' in a bold sans-serif (Helvetica Bold-like). Title size about 2.2 % of slide height (cap height about 1.6 %); body labels about 0.9 % of slide height; bullets about 0.8 %. Lines: thin (about 0.1 % of slide height) light-grey connectors with small arrowheads; dashed outlines (grey, fine dash) for component boxes; solid thin outlines for group boxes. Corner radii: outer frame about 1.5 % of slide width, group boxes square. No shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 61

**Text**

Nasdaq / Nasdaq Generative AI Platform / Gen AI Account / GPU Inference Account / Chat / Authorizer / Skill Connector / Skill Connector / High Code Skill / Hosted Skill / Data Container Connector / External Skill / Private VPC Endpoint / API Gateway / Elastic Load Balancer / Karpenter GPU Node Pool / NVIDIA L40 / EKS Cluster / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / NVIDIA LLM NIM / NeMo Guardrails / Model Image and Cache / NVIDIA GPU Operator / Elastic Block Storage / Data Container Management / Authorizer / Data Container API Gateway / Data Container Lambda / Data Container / Data Container Provisioner / pg_vector / Embedding Batch Job / Data Ingest / Nasdaq / Gitlab / Confluence / JIRA / Bucket

**Layout**

Full-slide black picture with the Nasdaq logo lockup at left (about 8 to 23 percent of width, vertically centred near 45 to 58 percent of height) and a single architecture diagram at right occupying about 38 to 95 percent of width and 21 to 79 percent of height inside a thin rounded frame. There is no title placeholder, footer, slide number or margin text beyond the diagram's own internal heading 'Nasdaq Generative AI Platform' at top-left of the frame.

**Visuals**

A system architecture diagram on black, with a Nasdaq logo at left. The diagram has a user icon at its left edge (a circled person outline) with a bracket line feeding two rows of the Gen AI Account. Top row (Chat box): Authorizer (Lambda icon) to Skill Connector (API-style icon) to Skill Connector (Lambda icon). Top-right box (High Code Skill): Hosted Skill (Lambda) to Data Container Connector (Lambda) to External Skill (API-style icon); a line runs down from the External Skill box to the Data Container box. Bottom-left box (Data Container Management): Authorizer (Lambda) to Data Container API Gateway (API-style icon) to Data Container Lambda (Lambda). Bottom-right box (Data Container): Data Container Provisioner (pink icon) to pg_vector (blue cylinder database icon) to Embedding Batch Job (orange icon), with the Embedding Batch Job connected upward to Private VPC Endpoint (purple cloud icon). A line from the Data Ingest box rises to the Data Container box. Data Ingest dashed box contains five icons in a row: Nasdaq logo, Gitlab fox logo, Confluence logo, JIRA logo, and a green Bucket icon, each with its label. The GPU Inference Account box contains API Gateway (pink icon) to Elastic Load Balancer (purple icon) with a connector down to an EKS Cluster dashed box holding NVIDIA NeMo Retriever, NVIDIA LLM NIM and NeMo Guardrails icons (green NVIDIA-style icons), plus NVIDIA GPU Operator (green chip icon) below. A dashed box at right contains Karpenter GPU Node Pool (blue Kubernetes-style hexagon icon) and NVIDIA L40 (green GPU card icon). Model Image and Cache is a dashed-outline box connected by a line to Elastic Block Storage (green icon with database and arrows). Icons are line-art in orange, pink, purple, blue and green on black; the Nasdaq logo mark is blue #0090BA with white wordmark. Lighting is flat; no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide picture covers an underlying white #FFFFFF fill). Pure black field; a white thin frame line and dashed grey sub-boxes; title and labels in white sans-serif (Helvetica-like regular for labels, semi-bold for the frame heading). The Nasdaq wordmark is a white geometric sans-serif (Nasdaq Sans style) with the blue #0090BA slanted parallelogram mark. Heading text is about 2.2 percent of slide height; diagram labels about 1.1 percent. Lines are thin white or light grey strokes about 1 px; frame corner radius small and rounded; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 62

**Text**

SAP Business AI / SAP APPLICATIONS / Joule / Cloud ERP / CRM / Spend / HXM / ... / Customer / Partner Apps / Development Tooling / Generative AI Hub in SAP AI Launchpad / Libraries / SAP BUSINESS TECHNOLOGY PLATFORM / Generative AI Hub / Trust / Data Masking / Content Moderation / Orchestration / Harmonized API / Prompt Registry / Grounding / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Access / Serving/Inference Engine / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / NVIDIA NIM / Llama Nemotron Reason (eval) / Foundation Models provided by Partners / Foundation Models built by SAP / Open source Foundation Models / Remote Foundation Models / Cloud ERP Data / CRM Data / Spend Data / HXM Data / ... / Vector Engine / Knowledge Graph / SAP HANA Cloud / SAP Business Data Cloud

**Layout**

Slide has no title placeholder and no footer or slide number. The SAP logo sits at the left-centre, vertically around the middle of the slide, and a large boxed architecture diagram occupies the right half, starting about 21% from the top and ending about 79% down, with its left edge near 38% of the width. The diagram's own heading 'SAP Business AI' is at its top-left inside the box. Content is left-aligned inside the diagram and the rest of the slide is empty black.

**Visuals**

Diagram (embedded image, 2295 by 1292 px, boxed by a thin light-grey rounded outline): an architecture diagram titled 'SAP Business AI'. Top block: a dark-blue box 'SAP APPLICATIONS' containing a full-width violet bar 'Joule' with a diamond icon, and four blue buttons 'Cloud ERP', 'CRM', 'Spend', 'HXM' and a small '...' button. Right of it: dark-grey pills 'Customer' and 'Partner Apps' joined by a dashed line, with a short vertical line and arrow down from Customer to a blue SAP BUSINESS TECHNOLOGY PLATFORM container. Inside the platform container: a light-blue 'Development Tooling' column holding boxes 'Generative AI Hub in SAP AI Launchpad' and 'Libraries', linked by an arrow to a violet 'Generative AI Hub' container with three sub-boxes: 'Trust' (Data Masking, Content Moderation), 'Orchestration' (Harmonized API, Prompt Registry, and a 'Grounding' box with an NVIDIA NeMo Retriever icon) and 'Access'. Arrows run from Access to a green-outlined 'Serving/Inference Engine' box holding three icons labelled 'NVIDIA NeMo Retriever', 'NVIDIA NIM' and 'Llama Nemotron Reason (eval)', and three blue bars 'Foundation Models provided by Partners', 'Foundation Models built by SAP', 'Open source Foundation Models'. A line from the Access connector runs down to a blue box 'Remote Foundation Models' outside the platform. Below the Generative AI Hub, a double-headed vertical arrow links to a data band with four blue buttons 'Cloud ERP Data', 'CRM Data', 'Spend Data', 'HXM Data' plus '...', then 'Vector Engine' and 'Knowledge Graph' bars, and a footer bar 'SAP HANA Cloud / SAP Business Data Cloud'. Diagram colours: navy #0B2A7A, royal blue #0A5BD6, bright blue #1F8CFF, violet #6A3CF5, dark navy #061A4A, green outline #7ED321, white sans-serif labels. Flat style, no shadows. Logo: the SAP wordmark, white bold letters on a blue (#0A8FDB) parallelogram with a diagonal cut at its lower right, not cropped, placed on black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide background, with a white #FFFFFF fill layer beneath the full-bleed image. Pure black background with no gradient. Diagram text is small white sans-serif (close to Helvetica/Arial, regular and bold), roughly 1.2% of slide height for box labels and 2% for the 'SAP Business AI' heading. Heading colour white #FFFFFF. Accent colours: blue #0A8FDB for logo, #1F8CFF for buttons, violet #6A3CF5 for Joule and Generative AI Hub, green #7ED321 outline for the Serving/Inference Engine. Shapes are square-cornered with small radii, thin light-grey rounded border around the whole diagram, no shadows.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a two-zone composition: the white ServiceNow wordmark sits vertically centred in the left column (about x 7 to 32 percent), and a single bordered rounded panel holding the 'ServiceNow AI Agents' architecture diagram fills the right column (about x 37.8 to 95.2 percent, y 21.3 to 78.8 percent). The panel title sits top-left inside the panel. No footer, page number or slide title sits outside the panel.

**Visuals**

One architecture diagram (flowchart on black, rendered as a raster image) inside a rounded-corner panel outlined in thin light grey #C8C8C8. Shapes and labels: (1) 'Triggers' box, thin white outline, containing two sub-columns: 'Primary' with a speech-bubble icon and 'Secondary' with an envelope icon and a database cylinder icon, separated by a vertical white divider; a white circular person icon with a green check badge overlaps its top-right corner. (2) A connector labelled 'User Message' in green text runs from Triggers to the central workflow box. (3) Central 'ServiceNow Agentic Workflow' box with a bright green #3DDC25 outline and soft green outer glow, containing a dark pill reading 'Autonomous, adaptive, goal-driven intelligence'. (4) 'Context' box (teal outline) with a dark pill 'Short & Long-term Memory' where 'Short', 'Long-term' are green. (5) 'Knowledge' box (teal-blue outline) with a green hexagonal network-hierarchy icon labelled 'NVIDIA NeMo' and a dark pill 'Internal & External Retrieval' where 'Internal' and 'External' are green. (6) 'LLM (Planners)' box (dark red outline) with a white cloud-brain icon on a green base labelled 'Llama Nemotron Reasoning' and two dark pills 'GPT4+' and 'Llama 3.3-70b'. (7) 'LLM (Execution)' box (red outline) with a green-and-black NVIDIA NIM cube icon labelled 'NVIDIA NIM' and one dark pill 'NowLLM'. (8) 'Tools' box (gold outline) with an NVIDIA Blueprint cube icon labelled 'NVIDIA Blueprint' and three dark pills 'Workflows', 'API's & Spokes', 'GenAI Skills'. (9) 'Action' small white-outlined box between Tools and the workflow. (10) 'Agent Collaboration' box (blue-violet outline) with two green-and-white head-with-circuit icons connected by dotted lines, and a row of four logos: a Splunk tile (orange-pink gradient with 'splunk>' text), a Snowflake-style red layers tile (white), a blue tile with a white 'W', and a blue tile with 'XM' (Qualtrics-style); followed by the text 'And more...'. Connectors are thin dotted or solid green and white orthogonal lines with small arrowheads, linking Triggers to Agent Collaboration (down-left), the workflow to Tools (down, splitting), the workflow to Action, Action to Tools (dotted), the workflow to LLM (Planners) and LLM (Execution) (right, splitting), and the workflow to Knowledge and Context (up-right). The ServiceNow wordmark is a white sans-serif logo with a green lowercase 'o' rendered as a swoosh, with a small white registered mark at lower right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (full-bleed black image covering the slide).

### Slide 64

**Text**

ROBOTS – THE NEXT MULTI-TRILLION DOLLAR INDUSTRY
SIMULATE
TRAIN
DEPLOY

**Layout**

Free-form diagram with no grid or columns. The title is centred horizontally across the top third, at about 17.7% to 20.6% of slide height. Three white elliptical orbit rings form a triangle: SIMULATE upper-left, TRAIN upper-right, and DEPLOY bottom-centre, with green double arrows linking them. No footer, slide number or logo is visible. Margins are generous, roughly 10% on the sides.

**Visuals**

Diagram of three hand-sketched chalk-style stations. (1) SIMULATE: a green swirling sphere, with a small white humanoid robot standing inside its centre and waving with its right arm, sitting on a white elliptical orbit ring. (2) TRAIN: on a white elliptical orbit ring, two small rounded-square boxes on the left each have an arrow pointing right to a vertical column of about six small squares; both columns feed a large white-outlined rounded rectangle filled with a gold-brown brush stroke; a small arrow leads from the rectangle to a second small column of squares, then to a white humanoid robot waving with its right arm. (3) DEPLOY: a blue and green Earth globe showing the Americas in white outline, with a white humanoid robot standing in front of its right edge, waving with its right hand, on a white elliptical orbit ring. Connectors: a green double-headed arrow between SIMULATE and TRAIN at the top centre; a green curved arrow with arrowheads at both ends running from SIMULATE down to DEPLOY on the left; a green curved arrow with arrowheads at both ends running from TRAIN down to DEPLOY on the right. Style: chalk and charcoal brush strokes, with a painterly glow.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background, with no photograph or pattern behind the artwork. Black background. Title in white #FFFFFF, uppercase, thin geometric hand-lettered sans-serif, regular weight, cap height about 3.2% of slide height. Stage labels in white #FFFFFF, same typeface, uppercase, about 2.8% of slide height. Accents are bright green #76B900 for the swirl, arrows and rings, with gold-brown #B5A050 for the brush strokes and a blue #1E5FA8 for the Earth. Lines are white chalk strokes about 0.5% of slide height thick with rough edges. No shadows, no rounded panel corners beyond the sketched rectangle, no gradients on the slide background.

### Slide 65

**Text**

A1

**Layout**

Two photographs sit side by side at equal height on a near-black field, inside a thin hairline frame that spans about 94.7% of the slide width. The left image occupies roughly the left half and the right image the right half, separated by a narrow black gutter of about 1.4% of the width. There is no title, footer, slide number or other text, and the margins are about 3.3% top, 2.7% left and 3.8% right and bottom.

**Visuals**

Two photographs. Left photograph: a woman with shoulder-length light-brown wavy hair and a cream knit sweater, wearing khaki trousers, stands at a white quartz kitchen sink and rinses a dark frying pan under a brushed-steel pull-down spring-neck faucet. Beside her stands a humanoid robot with a beige-taupe ribbed bodysuit, a domed head with a dark grey-tinted visor and a glowing white ring as its face, and jointed silver-grey hands reaching toward the sink; an open black dishwasher rack is in the lower foreground. Warm soft daylight enters from a window at the left, with white cabinets, a white vase of dried flowers and a dark gas hob at the back. Dominant colours are cream, beige, taupe, white and brushed steel. Composition is a medium shot cropped at the frame edges, with the robot in the right of the frame and the woman partly behind it. Right photograph: a close, shallow-focus view of a white humanoid robot head with a perforated speaker grille on its side, two small dark sensor lenses, and a black neck and shoulder assembly; its chest panel is dark with the label 'A1' and a small circle mark. A blurred dark blue-grey robot limb fills the left and foreground, and a blurred warehouse with metal shelving and a grey concrete floor forms the background. Lighting is cool, neutral and diffuse, with soft highlights on the white plastics. Dominant colours are white, grey, charcoal and dark blue-grey. Both photographs are boxed rectangles with square corners, not full-bleed, and neither has an overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid near-black #000000 (the white #FFFFFF fill beneath is hidden by a full-slide image). Near-black background with no gradient or pattern. No typefaces are used apart from the 'A1' marking printed on the robot's chest panel, which is a bold geometric sans-serif in white. The thin hairline frame is pale warm grey-gold, about #C9B99A, at roughly 0.5 pt. Photographs have square corners and no shadows. Image layers beneath the photos are a full-slide white #FFFFFF fill and a full-slide image, both covered by the black surround.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with no visible margins or frame. A hand-drawn L-shaped axis sits at the left, with a horizontal row of four stage labels (PERCEPTION AI, GENERATIVE AI, AGENTIC AI, PHYSICAL AI) along its foot, joined by arrows. The title FROM ONE TO THREE SCALING LAWS is centred in the upper-left third, above the rising green curve. Three circular images sit to the right of the curve, the largest at upper right, and the lower-right area is empty. No footer, page number, or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a hand-drawn scaling-law chart with a vertical axis labelled “INTELLIGENCE” (no numeric scale, no units) and a horizontal progression of four stages labelled PERCEPTION AI, GENERATIVE AI, AGENTIC AI, PHYSICAL AI, connected by white hand-drawn arrows. A green stroke rises from near PERCEPTION AI to the upper right, with three green arrowheads: a lower one labelled PRE-TRAINING SCALING, a middle one labelled POST-TRAINING SCALING, and an upper one labelled TEST-TIME SCALING “LONG THINKING”. Three circular images: (1) lower-centre circle, a 4x4 grid of first-person-view photographs of hands working in kitchens and workspaces, tinted cool grey-blue, labelled Internet Video Pre-Training; (2) small circle overlapping its upper right, showing a dark grey mechanical gripper on the left and a smiling person wearing a dark VR headset in a blue top on the right, against yellow shelving, labelled Human Demonstration; (3) large upper-right circle, a rendered 3D still of a light blue humanoid robot with a white-grey head and cyan ring on its chest, standing at a counter in a pastel kitchen with mint-green wall, lime and pink cabinets, blue countertop, a tan bowl, a teal kettle, a pink cup and a wooden utensil holder. The robot is centred, front-facing, soft even studio-like lighting, full figure visible; the circle crops the room edges. Dominant colours of that image: mint green, pink, lime, light blue, tan. Its lower band shows a beige and magenta gradient overlay carrying the labels Post-Training and Synthetic Data Generation and RL in Physics Sim.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000). Titles and stage labels are white (#FFFFFF) in an uppercase hand-drawn, chalk-like rounded sans-serif with irregular stroke edges, and the body captions beneath the circles are white NVIDIA Sans Regular sans-serif in sentence case. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the scaling-law labels, the curve and its arrowheads. Lines are hand-drawn white strokes of roughly 0.3% of slide width with slight wobble; arrows are open chevron-filled heads. Circles have thin grey (#8C8C8C) outlines of about 0.1% width and no shadows. Title text is about 3.8% of slide height; stage labels about 3.0%; caption text about 1.5%.

### Slide 67

**Text**

NVIDIA Omniverse With Cosmos
Physical AI Digital Twin Operating System
NVIDIA Omniverse
Cosmos

**Layout**

Title and subtitle are centred at the top, with the title above the subtitle. Two equal rounded-corner image panels sit side by side in the middle band, with a small grey arrow between them and a label centred under each panel and each logo. The left panel spans about x 2.8 to 47.3 and y 25.4 to 78.8. The right panel spans about x 53.0 to 97.5 and y 25.4 to 78.8. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Left: a stylised 3D render of a pastel kitchen. The walls are mint green, the cabinets are pink, yellow-green and lavender, and the counter is lavender. A light-blue humanoid robot with a grey-green head and a blue ring on its chest reaches toward a peach bowl on a tan island with a pink base. Right: a photorealistic kitchen with white cabinets, grey walls, an orange wood countertop and a white stove. A chrome humanoid robot with a black visor head and a blue ring on its chest holds a green glass bowl on the counter. Both panels are full-colour, evenly lit, and framed at full width with no cropping of the subjects. A thin grey arrow with a small arrowhead points right from the left panel to the right panel. Below the left panel is the NVIDIA Omniverse logo, a bright green ring with a black centre. Below the right panel is the Cosmos icon, a black hexagon with a silver spiral galaxy and a gold sphere, on a green base.

### Slide 68

**Text**

Disney Research / NVIDIA / Google DeepMind / Newton / MuJoCo Solver / Disney Research Solver / Custom Solver / NVIDIA Warp / MuJoCo Playground / Other Simulator / Isaac Lab

**Layout**

Three partner logos sit in a horizontal row across the upper part of the slide, centred at about 30 percent from the top. Below them, a left-hand rounded outlined panel holds a vertical solver diagram, a narrow centre column holds the MuJoCo Playground thumbnail and the Other Simulator icon, and a larger rounded photo panel sits on the right with its caption 'Isaac Lab' beneath it. There is no title, page number, footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram and photograph. Left panel: a thin light-grey rounded rectangle containing a flow diagram. At the top is a Newton icon (yellow cube-and-hand glyph on dark square with green underline) labelled 'Newton'; a thin grey arrow points up into it from a horizontal bracket line that joins the tops of three solver icons below. The three solver icons are yellow gears on dark squares with green underlines, labelled 'MuJoCo Solver' (left), 'Disney Research Solver' (centre) and 'Custom Solver' (right). A second bracket joins their bottoms into a single line that drops with an arrow down to a yellow gear icon labelled 'NVIDIA Warp' at the bottom centre of the panel. Centre column: a small rectangular thumbnail labelled 'MuJoCo Playground' showing a white robot arm on a blue checkered floor against a dark blue background, with a thin light-grey outline; a double-headed horizontal arrow runs from the left panel edge to the thumbnail's left edge. A green square icon with four small squares and a plus sign is labelled 'Other Simulator'; a grey line runs from a vertical line down from the top connector and ends in an arrow at its left edge. A thin grey elbow connector runs from the centre column at about 37 percent height to an arrow pointing into the right photo. Right panel: a photograph of a small white-and-grey bipedal robot with a flat grey sensor head carrying three round lenses and a thin antenna, standing on a rocky, gravelly desert floor with sandstone mountains behind. Framing is medium, slightly low, centred in a rounded box with soft edges. Lighting is warm, directional sunlight with pale blue sky above. Dominant colours are tan, ochre, grey-white and pale blue. The photo is boxed, not cropped, with a thin light-grey border and rounded corners. Logos: Disney Research wordmark (white script 'Disney' with 'Research' in sans-serif), NVIDIA wordmark (white eye emblem with 'NVIDIA' in bold sans-serif), Google DeepMind logo (white spiral emblem with 'Google DeepMind' in sans-serif).

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed dark photograph of a desert floor with a near-black charcoal to dark brown overlay, colour approximately #1A1A1C at top and #0D0C0B at bottom. Dark, cinematic look. Background is a near-black charcoal top fading to very dark brown at the bottom, with a blurred desert floor texture showing faintly through. Logos and labels are white or near-white (#FFFFFF and #E6E6E6). Accents are NVIDIA green #76B900 (icon underlines, Other Simulator icon) and gold-yellow #F2B705 (solver gear icons). Lines and panel borders are light grey #9A9A9A at thin stroke, about 1 px. Typeface for labels is a clean sans-serif similar to NVIDIA Sans, regular weight. Label text is about 1.8 percent of slide height. Panel corners are rounded with radius about 2 percent of slide width. No shadows or gradient overlays beyond the dark background.

Photograph of a night-time desert landscape. Below it are dark dune silhouettes, and the bottom third is near-black with a faint brown-red tint on the lowest dunes. Lighting is dusk or moonless night with no visible light source. A thin gold outline (about #C8A96A, roughly 0.1 percent of slide width) frames the photo, with a slightly rounded corner radius. There is no overlay or text on the photo.

**Layout**

Centred two-line title sits in the upper third, with a dark central panel holding the processing pipeline. Left column holds the camera photo above a text prompt box, and the humanoid figure sits on the right. Pipeline runs left to right, with no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a top-down view of a wooden table with two black robot hands, one on each side, and several black and grey cylindrical objects in the centre; a yellow bin with an orange object sits at the left edge. The image is boxed with rounded corners and a thin grey outline, not full-bleed. Diagram: a flow of shapes. A stack of five small gold squares labelled Sensor Tokens has five grey arrows pointing right into the Vision Language Model box. Below it, a stack of four gold squares labelled Text Tokens has four arrows pointing right into the same box. A row of four small gold squares sits between the Vision Language Model box and the Diffusion Transformer Model box. The Diffusion Transformer Model box has four arrows pointing right into a stack of four gold squares labelled Action Tokens. A long grey arrow leads from the Action Tokens to the humanoid figure. Icon-like figure: a full-length grey humanoid mannequin with a dark glossy head, standing on a faint shadow, shown front-on and centred on black with soft lighting. Arrows from the photo box and the text box point right into the central panel.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide. Black background, dark charcoal panel #0D0D0D with rounded corners. Title text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Medium, a clean geometric sans-serif, about 3.3% of slide height, with the second line in a heavier weight. Body labels are white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 1.7% of slide height. Accent colour is gold, with the token squares and model boxes filled with a gold gradient from light #C9B98A at the top to darker #6B5E3A at the bottom. Model boxes have rounded corners and a thin light edge. Connecting arrows and prompt-box outlines are thin mid-grey #8A8A8A lines. No shadows except a faint floor shadow under the figure.
